002212         }));

002221 fn expected_command_dispatch_identity(
002222     db: &Connection,
002223     operation_id: &str,
002224     operation: &Value,
002225     request: &Value,
002226     route: &Value,
002227 ) -> Result<(
002228     Value,
002229     Option<String>,
002230     crate::runtime::batch::CommandReceiptFacts,
002231 )> {
002232     let attempt_id = model::text(request, "attempt_id")?;
002233     if operation["attempt_id"] != attempt_id {
002234         return Err(Error::new(
002235             "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
002236             "Command receipt does not match the dispatch Operation attempt",
002237         ));
002238     }
002239     let attempt = tasks::get_attempt(db, attempt_id)?;
002240     let mut frozen_input = request.clone();
002241     if operation["operation_contract"]["task_prompt"]["contract_revision"]
002242         == swarm_contracts::task_prompt::TASK_PROMPT_CONTRACT_REVISION
002243     {
002244         let effective: String = db.query_row(
002245             "SELECT effective_request_json FROM operations WHERE operation_id=?1",
002246             [operation_id],
002247             |row| row.get(0),
002248         )?;
002249         frozen_input["task_prompt"] = serde_json::to_value(task_prompt::load(
002250             &serde_json::from_str::<Value>(&effective)?,
002251             &attempt,
002252             model::text(request, "text")?,
002253         )?)?;
002254     } else {
002255         frozen_input["task_snapshot"] = attempt["task_snapshot"].clone();
002256     }
002257     let instruction = crate::runtime::batch::instruction(&frozen_input)?;
002258     let expected = crate::runtime::batch::command_receipt_facts(operation_id, &instruction);
002259     let requested_model = match route["native_options"].get("modelId") {
002260         None | Some(Value::Null) => None,
002261         Some(_) => Some(model::text(&route["native_options"], "modelId")?.to_owned()),
002262     };
002263     Ok((attempt, requested_model, expected))
002264 }

002266 fn command_target_receipt_fields(
002267     db: &Connection,
002268     operation_id: &str,
002269     operation: &Value,
002270     request: &Value,
002271     route: &Value,
002272     binding_id: &str,
002273     generation: i64,
002274 ) -> Result<Value> {
002275     if operation["method"] != "task.dispatch"
002276         || operation["binding_id"] != binding_id
002277         || operation["binding_generation"] != generation
002278     {
002279         return Err(Error::new(
002280             "FORBIDDEN",
002281             "reconcile target belongs to another binding generation or method",
002282         ));
002283     }
002284     let (attempt, requested_model, facts) =
002285         expected_command_dispatch_identity(db, operation_id, operation, request, route)?;
002286     if attempt["binding_id"] != binding_id || attempt["binding_generation"] != generation {
002287         return Err(Error::new(
002288             "FORBIDDEN",
002289             "reconcile target Attempt belongs to another binding generation",
002290         ));
002291     }
002292     Ok(json!({
002293         "target_command_method":"task.dispatch",
002294         "target_command_requested_model":requested_model,
002295         "target_command_core_binding":facts.as_json()
002296     }))
002297 }

002299 fn validate_command_dispatch_receipt(
002300     db: &Connection,
002301     operation: &Value,
002302     route: &Value,
002303     receipt: &RuntimeOutcome,
002304 ) -> Result<()> {
002305     let raw: String = db.query_row(
002306         "SELECT original_request_json FROM operations WHERE operation_id=?1",
002307         [&receipt.operation_id],
002308         |row| row.get(0),
002309     )?;
002310     let request: Value = serde_json::from_str(&raw)?;
002311     let (_attempt, expected_model, expected) =
002312         expected_command_dispatch_identity(db, &receipt.operation_id, operation, &request, route)?;
002313     if receipt.details["batch_run_id"] != expected.batch_run_id
002314         || receipt.details["prompt_sha256"] != expected.prompt_sha256
002315         || receipt.details["prompt_bytes"].as_u64() != u64::try_from(expected.prompt_bytes).ok()
002316         || receipt.details.get("requested_model") != Some(&json!(expected_model))
002317     {
002318         return Err(Error::new(
002319             "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
002320             "Command receipt differs from the core-frozen Operation and prompt identity",
002321         ));
002322     }
002323     Ok(())
002324 }

002749 pub(super) fn outcome_with_artifacts(
002750     db: &mut Connection,
002751     p: &Principal,
002752     v: &Value,
002753     artifacts: &[ArtifactRecord],
002754 ) -> Result<Value> {
002755     let r: RuntimeOutcome = serde_json::from_value(v.clone())?;
002756     let tx = db.transaction_with_behavior(TransactionBehavior::Immediate)?;
002757     let (id, generation, b) = scope(&tx, p, true)?;
002758     let pre_input_open = descriptor_pre_input_open(&tx, &b)?;
002759     let o = operations::get_operation(&tx, &r.operation_id)?;
002760     if o["binding_id"] != id || o["binding_generation"] != generation {
002761         return Err(Error::new(
002762             "FORBIDDEN",
002763             "operation belongs to another binding",
002764         ));
002765     }
002766     let module_owned_service = b["route"]["runtime"] == "module"
002767         && b["route"]["module_artifact_id"] == crate::config::OPENCODE_RUST_ARTIFACT_ID
002768         && b["route"]["owned_service"].is_object();
002769     let has_owned_service_ready = r
002770         .details
002771         .as_object()
002772         .is_some_and(|details| details.contains_key("owned_service_ready"));
002773     let has_ready_only_marker = r
002774         .details
002775         .as_object()
002776         .is_some_and(|details| details.contains_key("owned_service_ready_only"));
002777     let owned_service_ready_only = r.details["owned_service_ready_only"] == true;
002778     let valid_ready_only_marker = has_ready_only_marker == owned_service_ready_only;
002779     let valid_ready_outcome = (matches!(r.outcome, EffectOutcome::Applied)
002780         && !owned_service_ready_only)
002781         || (matches!(r.outcome, EffectOutcome::Rejected | EffectOutcome::Unknown)
002782             && owned_service_ready_only);
002783     let applied_open_requires_ready_receipt = module_owned_service
002784         && o["method"] == "agent.open"
002785         && matches!(r.outcome, EffectOutcome::Applied);
002786     if (has_owned_service_ready || has_ready_only_marker || applied_open_requires_ready_receipt)
002787         && !(valid_ready_only_marker
002788             && has_owned_service_ready
002789             && module_owned_service
002790             && o["method"] == "agent.open"
002791             && valid_ready_outcome)
002792     {
002793         return Err(Error::invalid(
002794             "owned service readiness must bind an applied or ready-only module agent.open",
002795         ));
002796     }
002797     // Versioned bindings require a typed receipt for every outcome. Legacy
002798     // unversioned bindings retain their existing validators and wire contract.
002799     let builtin_opencode_dispatch = crate::config::is_opencode_task_prompt_builtin_route(
002800         b["route"]["runtime"].as_str().unwrap_or_default(),
002801         b["module_artifact_id"].as_str().unwrap_or_default(),
002802     ) && o["method"] == "task.dispatch"
002803         && matches!(r.outcome, EffectOutcome::Applied | EffectOutcome::Accepted);
002804     let module_receipt = if b["observation"].get("module_contract_selector").is_some()
002805         || builtin_opencode_dispatch
002806         || o["method"] == swarm_contracts::module_command::COMMAND_RELEASE_EVIDENCE_METHOD
002807         || (zed::is_task_prompt_route(&b["route"])
002808             && o["method"] == "task.dispatch"
002809             && matches!(r.outcome, EffectOutcome::Applied | EffectOutcome::Accepted))
002810     {
002811         Some(validate_module_receipt_for_operation(
002812             &tx, &id, generation, &b, &r,
002813         )?)
002814     } else {
002815         None
002816     };
002817     let effective_raw: String = tx.query_row(
002818         "SELECT effective_request_json FROM operations WHERE operation_id=?1",
002819         [&r.operation_id],
002820         |row| row.get(0),
002821     )?;
002822     let effective: Value = serde_json::from_str(&effective_raw)?;
002823     let continuation_expected = o["method"] == "agent.send"
002824         && effective["automation_on_behalf"]["action"] == "agent.send"
002825         && effective["automation_on_behalf"]["cause"]["kind"] == "goal_progression";
002826     if continuation_expected {
002827         match r.outcome {
002828             EffectOutcome::Accepted | EffectOutcome::Applied => {
002829                 let receipt = module_receipt.as_ref().ok_or_else(|| {
002830                     Error::new(
002831                         "GOAL_CONTINUATION_ADMISSION_INVALID",
002832                         "Codex Goal continuation requires a typed module receipt",
002833                     )
002834                 })?;
002835                 validate_goal_continuation_admission(&tx, &id, generation, &b, &o, &r, receipt)?;
002836             }
002837             EffectOutcome::Rejected | EffectOutcome::Unknown => {
002838                 // A failed or transport-uncertain continuation still has an
002839                 // authenticated ordinary Operation outcome and must reach the
002840                 // existing rejection/unknown observation path. It cannot carry
002841                 // a receipt or terminal marker that would imply native input
002842                 // admission or completed Goal evidence.
002843                 if r.details.get("goal_continuation_admission").is_some()
002844                     || r.details.get("dispatch_admission").is_some()
002845                     || r.details
002846                         .get("goal_terminal_event")
002847                         .is_some_and(|value| !value.is_null())
002848                 {
002849                     return Err(Error::new(
002850                         "GOAL_CONTINUATION_ADMISSION_INVALID",
002851                         "failed or uncertain Goal continuation cannot carry admission or terminal evidence",
002852                     ));
002853                 }
002854             }
002855         }
002856     } else if r.details.get("goal_continuation_admission").is_some() {
002857         return Err(Error::new(
002858             "GOAL_CONTINUATION_ADMISSION_INVALID",
002859             "continuation receipt is outside a Store-authenticated Goal agent.send",
002860         ));
002861     }
002862     if has_owned_service_ready {
002863         super::launcher_owned_service::retain_module_owned_service_ready(
002864             &tx, &id, generation, &o, &r,
002865         )?;
002866     }
002867     // The normalized receipt is required only for a known native admission.
002868     // Unknown is deliberately left unresolved so it cannot create an Attempt
002869     // producer or imply Task completion.
002870     let normalized_dispatch_admission = if o["method"] == "task.dispatch"
002871         && selected_task_dispatch_admission(&tx, &b)?
002872         && matches!(r.outcome, EffectOutcome::Applied | EffectOutcome::Accepted)
002873     {
002874         let module_receipt = module_receipt.as_ref().ok_or_else(|| {
002875             Error::new(
002876                 "MODULE_RECEIPT_INVALID",
002877                 "normalized dispatch requires a typed module receipt",
002878             )
--- pub(super) fn result(: []
--- pub(super) fn result_with_artifacts(: []
--- validate_runtime_outcome_identity: []
--- bridge_boot_id: [224, 299, 648, 834, 837, 960, 961, 998, 1017, 1032, 1641, 3434, 3545, 5060, 5061, 5062, 5152, 5468, 6378]
@@ 192:353 @@
000192             "MODULE_DESCRIPTOR_MISSING",
000193             "selected binding has no retained module descriptor",
000194         )
000195     })?;
000196     let context = swarm_contracts::module_contract::task_dispatch_context_schema();
000197     let admission = swarm_contracts::module_contract::task_dispatch_admission_schema();
000198     let declares_context = identity.command_schemas.contains(&context);
000199     let declares_admission = identity.event_schemas.contains(&admission);
000200     if declares_context != declares_admission {
000201         return Err(Error::new(
000202             "MODULE_CONTRACT_INCOMPATIBLE",
000203             "normalized task dispatch requires both context and admission schemas",
000204         ));
000205     }
000206     Ok(declares_context)
000207 }

000209 fn task_dispatch_context(
000210     operation_id: &str,
000211     binding_id: &str,
000212     binding_generation: i64,
000213     binding: &Value,
000214     request: &Value,
000215     attempt: &Value,

000219     Ok(swarm_contracts::runtime::TaskDispatchContext {
000220         schema_version: 1,
000221         operation_id: operation_id.to_owned(),
000222         binding_id: binding_id.to_owned(),
000223         binding_generation,
000224         worker_boot_id: model::text(&binding["observation"], "bridge_boot_id")?.to_owned(),

000226         task_id: model::text(attempt, "task_id")?.to_owned(),
000227         task_revision: model::positive(attempt, "task_revision")?,
000228         task_snapshot_sha256: model::digest(model::canonical(snapshot)?.as_bytes()),
000229         source_text_sha256: model::digest(text.as_bytes()),
000230         source_text_bytes: u64::try_from(text.len())
000231             .map_err(|_| Error::invalid("task dispatch text length is out of range"))?,
000232     })
000233 }

000235 fn retained_task_dispatch_context(

000254 fn goal_continuation_admission_context(
000255     db: &Connection,
000256     operation_id: &str,
000257     binding_id: &str,
000258     binding_generation: i64,
000259     binding: &Value,
000260     effective: &Value,
000261 ) -> Result<Option<GoalContinuationAdmissionContext>> {
000262     let on_behalf = &effective["automation_on_behalf"];
000263     if on_behalf["action"] != "agent.send" || on_behalf["cause"]["kind"] != "goal_progression" {
000264         return Ok(None);
000265     }
000266     if selected_codex_controller_contract(db, binding)?.is_none() {
000267         return Err(Error::new(
000268             "GOAL_UNSUPPORTED_RUNTIME",
000269             "only the Codex controller has a closed Goal continuation admission receipt",
000270         ));
000271     }
000272     let continuation: GoalContinuationLink =
000273         serde_json::from_value(on_behalf["cause"]["continuation"].clone()).map_err(|_| {
000274             Error::new(
000275                 "AUTOMATION_LINK_CORRUPT",
000276                 "Goal continuation admission is missing its immutable linkage",
000277             )
000278         })?;
000279     if continuation.validate().is_err()
000280         || continuation.method != "agent.send"
000281         || continuation.source_operation_id
000282             != on_behalf["cause"]["source_operation_id"]
000283                 .as_str()
000284                 .unwrap_or_default()
000285         || continuation.source_observation_id
000286             != on_behalf["cause"]["source_observation_id"]
000287                 .as_i64()
000288                 .unwrap_or_default()
000289         || serde_json::to_value(&continuation.terminal_event)?
000290             != on_behalf["cause"]["terminal_event"]
000291         || continuation.binding_id != binding_id
000292         || continuation.binding_generation != binding_generation
000293     {
000294         return Err(Error::new(
000295             "AUTOMATION_LINK_CORRUPT",
000296             "Goal continuation admission linkage differs from its retained cause",
000297         ));
000298     }
000299     let worker_boot_id = model::text(&binding["observation"], "bridge_boot_id")?.to_owned();
000300     let context = GoalContinuationAdmissionContext {
000301         schema_id: swarm_contracts::module_contract::GOAL_CONTINUATION_CONTEXT_SCHEMA_ID.to_owned(),
000302         schema_version: GoalContinuationAdmissionContext::VERSION,
000303         operation_id: operation_id.to_owned(),
000304         binding_id: binding_id.to_owned(),
000305         binding_generation,
000306         worker_boot_id,
000307         continuation,
000308     };
000309     context.validate().map_err(|_| {
000310         Error::new(
000311             "AUTOMATION_LINK_CORRUPT",
000312             "Goal continuation context is invalid",
000313         )
000314     })?;
000315     Ok(Some(context))
000316 }

000318 fn validate_task_dispatch_admission(
000319     db: &Connection,
000320     binding_id: &str,
000321     binding_generation: i64,
000322     binding: &Value,
000323     operation: &Value,
000324     outcome: &RuntimeOutcome,
000325     module_receipt: &swarm_contracts::runtime::ModuleReceiptIdentity,
000326 ) -> Result<swarm_contracts::runtime::TaskDispatchAdmissionReceipt> {
000327     if let Some(contract) = selected_codex_controller_contract(db, binding)?
000328         && (module_receipt.module_id != contract.identity.module_id
000329             || module_receipt.artifact != contract.identity.artifact
000330             || module_receipt.protocol != contract.identity.protocol)
000331     {
000332         return Err(Error::new(
000333             "TASK_DISPATCH_ADMISSION_INVALID",
000334             "Codex dispatch receipt differs from the selected controller contract",
000335         ));
000336     }
000337     if model::text(operation, "method")? != "task.dispatch"
000338         || model::text(operation, "operation_id")? != outcome.operation_id
000339     {
000340         return Err(Error::new(
000341             "TASK_DISPATCH_ADMISSION_INVALID",
000342             "admission receipt must name its exact task.dispatch Operation",
000343         ));
000344     }
000345     let value = outcome.details.get("dispatch_admission").ok_or_else(|| {
000346         Error::new(
000347             "TASK_DISPATCH_ADMISSION_INVALID",
000348             "normalized dispatch admission receipt is missing",
000349         )
000350     })?;
000351     let receipt: swarm_contracts::runtime::TaskDispatchAdmissionReceipt =
000352         serde_json::from_value(value.clone()).map_err(|_| {
000353             Error::new(
000354                 "TASK_DISPATCH_ADMISSION_INVALID",
000355                 "normalized dispatch admission receipt has an invalid shape",
000356             )
000357         })?;
000358     receipt.validate().map_err(|_| {
000359         Error::new(
000360             "TASK_DISPATCH_ADMISSION_INVALID",
000361             "normalized dispatch admission receipt is invalid",
000362         )
000363     })?;
000364     if receipt.module_receipt != *module_receipt
000365         || receipt.operation_id != outcome.operation_id
000366         || receipt.binding_id != binding_id
000367         || receipt.binding_generation != binding_generation
000368         || receipt.native_input_id != outcome.native_input_id
000369     {
000370         return Err(Error::new(
000371             "TASK_DISPATCH_ADMISSION_INVALID",
000372             "normalized dispatch receipt names another module, boot, binding, Operation, or native input",
000373         ));
000374     }
000375 
000376     let raw: String = db.query_row(
000377         "SELECT original_request_json FROM operations WHERE operation_id=?1 AND binding_id=?2 AND binding_generation=?3",
000378         params![outcome.operation_id.as_str(), binding_id, binding_generation],
000379         |row| row.get(0),
000380     )?;
000381     let request: Value = serde_json::from_str(&raw).map_err(|_| {
000382         Error::new(
000383             "TASK_DISPATCH_ADMISSION_INVALID",
000384             "stored task.dispatch request is malformed",
000385         )
000386     })?;
000387     let attempt_id = model::text(&request, "attempt_id")?;
000388     if receipt.attempt_id != attempt_id || model::text(operation, "attempt_id")? != attempt_id {
000389         return Err(Error::new(
000390             "TASK_DISPATCH_ADMISSION_INVALID",
000391             "normalized dispatch receipt names another Attempt",
000392         ));
000393     }
000394     let attempt = tasks::get_attempt(db, attempt_id)?;
000395     if attempt["binding_id"] != binding_id
000396         || attempt["binding_generation"] != binding_generation
000397         || attempt["start_operation_id"] != outcome.operation_id
000398     {
000399         return Err(Error::new(
000400             "TASK_DISPATCH_ADMISSION_INVALID",
000401             "normalized dispatch receipt does not match the retained Attempt owner",
000402         ));
000403     }
000404     let effective_raw: String = db.query_row(
000405         "SELECT effective_request_json FROM operations WHERE operation_id=?1",
000406         [&outcome.operation_id],
000407         |row| row.get(0),
000408     )?;
000409     let effective: Value = serde_json::from_str(&effective_raw)?;
000410     let retained = retained_task_dispatch_context(&effective)?;
000411     let mut expected = task_dispatch_context(
000412         &outcome.operation_id,
000413         binding_id,
000414         binding_generation,
000415         binding,
000416         &request,
000417         &attempt,
000418     )?;
000419     // Recovery joins the admitted worker boot, even after a new host attach.
000420     // Every other field is checked against the immutable Operation and Attempt.
000421     expected.worker_boot_id = retained.worker_boot_id.clone();
000422     if retained != expected || receipt.context() != expected {
000423         return Err(Error::new(
000424             "TASK_DISPATCH_ADMISSION_INVALID",
000425             "normalized dispatch receipt differs from the original text or immutable Task snapshot",
000426         ));
000427     }
000428     if task_prompt::selected(db, binding)? {
@@ 616:777 @@
000616             ));
000617         }
000618     } else {
000619         if current["observation"].get("module_client_id").is_some() {
000620             return Err(Error::new(
000621                 "MODULE_OWNER_MISMATCH",
000622                 "binding already has another module credential",
000623             ));
000624         }
000625         register(
000626             &tx,
000627             &json!({"binding_id":id,"binding_generation":generation}),
000628             &client,
000629         )?;
000630         set_meta(
000631             &tx,
000632             &format!("client:{client}"),
000633             &json!({"role":"module","disabled":false,"builtin_runtime":zed::RUNTIME,"binding_id":id,"binding_generation":generation}),
000634         )?;
000635     }
000636     let principal = Principal {
000637         client_id: client,
000638         link_id: model::new_id(),
000639         role: Role::Module,
000640     };
000641     let now = model::now_ms()?;
000642     tx.execute(
000643         "UPDATE operations SET state='outcome_unknown',updated_at_ms=?3 WHERE binding_id=?1 AND binding_generation=?2 AND state IN ('sending','native_accepted') AND method IN ('agent.open','task.dispatch','agent.refresh','agent.reconcile','agent.result','native.mcp.install','native.mcp.observe','native.mcp.arm','native.mcp.read')",
000644         params![id, generation, now],
000645     )?;
000646     super::capacity::sync_binding(&tx, id, generation, now)?;
000647     tx.execute(
000648         "UPDATE bindings SET state=CASE WHEN COALESCE(json_extract(state_json,'$.recovery_required'),0)=1 OR EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM operations WHERE binding_id=?1 AND binding_generation=?2 AND state IN ('sending','native_accepted','outcome_unknown') AND method IN ('agent.open','task.dispatch')) THEN 'reconciling' WHEN state='reconciling' THEN 'ready' ELSE state END,state_json=json_set(state_json,'$.bridge_boot_id',?3,'$.module_link_id',?4,'$.connection','connected','$.native_owner','controller_local_executor') WHERE binding_id=?1 AND generation=?2",
000649         params![id, generation, boot, principal.link_id],
000650     )?;
000651     tx.commit()?;
000652     Ok(principal)
000653 }
000654 
000655 /// Restore the frozen dispatch fields for native readback and reconciliation.
000656 pub(super) fn restore_task_dispatch_input(
000657     db: &Connection,
000658     operation: &Value,
000659     input: &mut Value,
000660 ) -> Result<()> {
000661     let attempt = tasks::get_attempt(db, model::text(input, "attempt_id")?)?;
000662     if operation["operation_contract"]["task_prompt"]["contract_revision"]
000663         == swarm_contracts::task_prompt::TASK_PROMPT_CONTRACT_REVISION
000664     {
000665         let effective: String = db.query_row(
000666             "SELECT effective_request_json FROM operations WHERE operation_id=?1",
000667             [model::text(operation, "operation_id")?],
000668             |row| row.get(0),
000669         )?;
000670         let effective: Value = serde_json::from_str(&effective)?;
000671         input["task_prompt"] = serde_json::to_value(task_prompt::load(
000672             &effective,
000673             &attempt,
000674             model::text(input, "text")?,
000675         )?)?;
000676         input["task_dispatch_context"] =
000677             serde_json::to_value(retained_task_dispatch_context(&effective)?)?;
000678     } else {
000679         input["task_snapshot"] = attempt["task_snapshot"].clone();
000680     }
000681     Ok(())
000682 }
000683 
000684 fn batch_original(
000685     db: &Connection,
000686     principal: &Principal,
000687     operation_id: &str,
000688 ) -> Result<RuntimeCommand> {
000689     let (binding_id, generation, binding) = scope(db, principal, true)?;
000690     let operation = operations::get_operation(db, operation_id)?;
000691     if operation["binding_id"] != binding_id || operation["binding_generation"] != generation {
000692         return Err(Error::new(
000693             "FORBIDDEN",
000694             "batch operation belongs to another binding",
000695         ));
000696     }
000697     let raw: String = db.query_row(
000698         "SELECT original_request_json FROM operations WHERE operation_id=?1",
000699         [operation_id],
000700         |row| row.get(0),
000701     )?;
000702     let mut input: Value = serde_json::from_str(&raw)?;
000703     let input_sha256 = model::digest(model::canonical(&input)?.as_bytes());
000704     let method = model::text(&operation, "method")?.to_owned();
000705     if method == "task.dispatch" {
000706         restore_task_dispatch_input(db, &operation, &mut input)?;
000707     }
000708     Ok(RuntimeCommand {
000709         operation_id: operation_id.to_owned(),
000710         method,
000711         created_at_ms: model::positive(&operation, "created_at_ms")?,
000712         binding_id,
000713         generation,
000714         native_root_id: None,
000715         route: binding["route"].clone(),
000716         input,
000717         input_sha256: Some(input_sha256),
000718         target_input_sha256: None,
000719     })
000720 }
000721 
000722 pub(super) fn scope(
000723     db: &Connection,
000724     p: &Principal,
000725     check_link: bool,
000726 ) -> Result<(String, i64, Value)> {
000727     if p.role != Role::Module {
000728         return Err(Error::new("FORBIDDEN", "module credential required"));
000729     }
000730     let c = meta(db, &format!("client:{}", p.client_id))?
000731         .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("UNAUTHORIZED", "module is not registered"))?;
000732     if c["disabled"] == true || c["role"] != "module" {
000733         return Err(Error::new("UNAUTHORIZED", "module is disabled"));
000734     }
000735     let id = model::text(&c, "binding_id")?.to_string();
000736     let generation = model::positive(&c, "binding_generation")?;
000737     let b = operations::get_binding(db, &id, generation)?;
000738     if !b["released_at_ms"].is_null() {
000739         return Err(Error::new("BINDING_CLOSED", "module binding is released"));
000740     }
000741     if b["observation"]["module_client_id"] != p.client_id {
000742         return Err(Error::new(
000743             "MODULE_OWNER_MISMATCH",
000744             "credential does not own this module binding",
000745         ));
000746     }
000747     if check_link && b["observation"]["module_link_id"] != p.link_id {
000748         return Err(Error::new(
000749             "STALE_LINK",
000750             "reconnect and reconcile this module before new commands",
000751         ));
000752     }
000753     Ok((id, generation, b))
000754 }
000755 
000756 /// Permit an already-admitted normalized page to be persisted or replayed
000757 /// after a binding release or module-link change. This grants no new command;
000758 /// the exact Operation origin is checked by normalized_result::validate_source.
000759 pub(super) fn admitted_result_scope(
000760     db: &Connection,
000761     p: &Principal,
000762     operation_id: &str,
000763 ) -> Result<(String, i64, Value)> {
000764     if p.role != Role::Module {
000765         return Err(Error::new("FORBIDDEN", "module credential required"));
000766     }
000767     let client = meta(db, &format!("client:{}", p.client_id))?
000768         .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("UNAUTHORIZED", "module is not registered"))?;
000769     if client["role"] != "module" {
000770         return Err(Error::new("UNAUTHORIZED", "client is not a module"));
000771     }
000772     let id = model::text(&client, "binding_id")?.to_owned();
000773     let generation = model::positive(&client, "binding_generation")?;
000774     let operation = operations::get_operation(db, operation_id)?;
000775     let effective_raw: String = db.query_row(
000776         "SELECT effective_request_json FROM operations WHERE operation_id=?1",
000777         [operation_id],
@@ 802:963 @@
000802             "MODULE_OWNER_MISMATCH",
000803             "credential does not own this registered module binding",
000804         ));
000805     }
000806     Ok((id, generation, binding))
000807 }
000808 
000809 pub(super) fn register(db: &Connection, v: &Value, client_id: &str) -> Result<Value> {
000810     let id = model::text(v, "binding_id")?;
000811     let generation = model::positive(v, "binding_generation")?;
000812     let b = operations::get_binding(db, id, generation)?;
000813     if !b["released_at_ms"].is_null() {
000814         return Err(Error::new(
000815             "BINDING_CLOSED",
000816             "cannot register a released binding",
000817         ));
000818     }
000819     if b["observation"].get("module_client_id").is_some() {
000820         return Err(Error::conflict("binding already has a module credential"));
000821     }
000822     db.execute("UPDATE bindings SET state_json=json_set(state_json,'$.module_client_id',?3) WHERE binding_id=?1 AND generation=?2", params![id,generation,client_id])?;
000823     Ok(json!({"binding_id":id,"binding_generation":generation}))
000824 }

000826 pub(super) fn hello_plan(db: &Connection, p: &Principal, v: &Value) -> Result<Value> {
000827     let (_, _, b) = scope(db, p, false)?;
000828     let module_contract_negotiation = super::module_handshake::negotiate_hello(
000829         db,
000830         model::text(&b, "module_artifact_id")?,
000831         b["observation"].get("module_contract_selector"),
000832         v.get("module_contract"),
000833     )?;
000834     let changed = b["observation"]["bridge_boot_id"]
000835         .as_str()
000836         .is_some_and(|old| Some(old) != v["boot_id"].as_str());
000837     Ok(json!({"old_boot":b["observation"]["bridge_boot_id"],
000838         "owner":b["observation"]["managed_owner"],"changed":changed,
000839         "module_contract_negotiation":module_contract_negotiation}))
000840 }

000862 fn hello_internal(
000863     db: &mut Connection,
000864     p: &Principal,
000865     v: &Value,
000866     verified: &Value,
000867     config: Option<&crate::config::Config>,
000868 ) -> Result<Value> {
000869     model::fields(
000870         v,
000871         &[
000872             "boot_id",
000873             "module_artifact_id",
000874             "module_contract",
000875             "native_root_id",
000876             "native_scope_key",
000877             "native_ready",
000878             "managed_owner",
000879         ],
000880     )?;
000881     let boot = model::text(v, "boot_id")?;
000882     let artifact = model::text(v, "module_artifact_id")?;
000883     let tx = db.transaction_with_behavior(TransactionBehavior::Immediate)?;
000884     let (id, generation, b) = scope(&tx, p, false)?;
000885     let pre_input_open = descriptor_pre_input_open(&tx, &b)?;
000886     if b["route"]["module_artifact_id"] != artifact {
000887         return Err(Error::new(
000888             "ARTIFACT_MISMATCH",
000889             "module artifact differs from the reserved route",
000890         ));
000891     }
000892     let module_contract_negotiation = super::module_handshake::negotiate_hello(
000893         &tx,
000894         model::text(&b, "module_artifact_id")?,
000895         b["observation"].get("module_contract_selector"),
000896         v.get("module_contract"),
000897     )?;
000898     if verified.get("module_contract_negotiation") != Some(&module_contract_negotiation) {
000899         return Err(Error::new(
000900             "STALE_MODULE_CONTRACT",
000901             "trusted module descriptor changed during hello preflight",
000902         ));
000903     }
000904     let sessionless_batch = crate::runtime::batch::is_sessionless_route(&b["route"]);
000905     let requested_root = optional_identity_text(v, "native_root_id")?;
000906     let requested_scope = optional_identity_text(v, "native_scope_key")?;
000907     if requested_root.is_some() != requested_scope.is_some() {
000908         return Err(Error::new(
000909             "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000910             "native root and scope must be supplied together",
000911         ));
000912     }
000913     let retained_root = optional_identity_text(&b, "native_root_id")?;
000914     let retained_scope = optional_identity_text(&b, "native_scope_key")?;
000915     if retained_root.is_some() != retained_scope.is_some() {
000916         return Err(Error::new(
000917             "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000918             "Store retained an incomplete native identity pair",
000919         ));
000920     }
000921     if sessionless_batch && (requested_root.is_some() || retained_root.is_some()) {
000922         return Err(Error::new(
000923             "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000924             "sessionless batch bindings cannot claim a native root or scope",
000925         ));
000926     }
000927     match (
000928         retained_root,
000929         retained_scope,
000930         requested_root,
000931         requested_scope,
000932     ) {
000933         (
000934             Some(retained_root),
000935             Some(retained_scope),
000936             Some(requested_root),
000937             Some(requested_scope),
000938         ) if retained_root != requested_root || retained_scope != requested_scope => {
000939             return Err(Error::new(
000940                 "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000941                 "supplied native identity differs from the Store-owned pair",
000942             ));
000943         }
000944         (None, None, Some(_), Some(_)) => {
000945             return Err(Error::new(
000946                 "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000947                 "module hello cannot introduce a native identity that Store has not retained",
000948             ));
000949         }
000950         (Some(_), Some(_), None, None)
000951         | (Some(_), Some(_), Some(_), Some(_))
000952         | (None, None, None, None) => {}
000953         _ => {
000954             return Err(Error::new(
000955                 "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000956                 "native root and scope must be complete identity pairs",
000957             ));
000958         }
000959     }
000960     let old_boot = b["observation"]["bridge_boot_id"].as_str();
000961     if verified["old_boot"] != b["observation"]["bridge_boot_id"]
000962         || verified["owner"] != b["observation"]["managed_owner"]
000963     {
@@ 805:966 @@
000805     }
000806     Ok((id, generation, binding))
000807 }

000809 pub(super) fn register(db: &Connection, v: &Value, client_id: &str) -> Result<Value> {
000810     let id = model::text(v, "binding_id")?;
000811     let generation = model::positive(v, "binding_generation")?;
000812     let b = operations::get_binding(db, id, generation)?;
000813     if !b["released_at_ms"].is_null() {
000814         return Err(Error::new(
000815             "BINDING_CLOSED",
000816             "cannot register a released binding",
000817         ));
000818     }
000819     if b["observation"].get("module_client_id").is_some() {
000820         return Err(Error::conflict("binding already has a module credential"));
000821     }
000822     db.execute("UPDATE bindings SET state_json=json_set(state_json,'$.module_client_id',?3) WHERE binding_id=?1 AND generation=?2", params![id,generation,client_id])?;
000823     Ok(json!({"binding_id":id,"binding_generation":generation}))
000824 }

000829         db,
000830         model::text(&b, "module_artifact_id")?,
000831         b["observation"].get("module_contract_selector"),
000832         v.get("module_contract"),

000835         .as_str()
000836         .is_some_and(|old| Some(old) != v["boot_id"].as_str());

000838         "owner":b["observation"]["managed_owner"],"changed":changed,

000840 }

000862 fn hello_internal(
000863     db: &mut Connection,
000864     p: &Principal,
000865     v: &Value,
000866     verified: &Value,
000867     config: Option<&crate::config::Config>,
000868 ) -> Result<Value> {
000869     model::fields(
000870         v,
000871         &[
000872             "boot_id",
000873             "module_artifact_id",
000874             "module_contract",
000875             "native_root_id",
000876             "native_scope_key",
000877             "native_ready",
000878             "managed_owner",
000879         ],
000880     )?;
000881     let boot = model::text(v, "boot_id")?;
000882     let artifact = model::text(v, "module_artifact_id")?;
000883     let tx = db.transaction_with_behavior(TransactionBehavior::Immediate)?;
000884     let (id, generation, b) = scope(&tx, p, false)?;
000885     let pre_input_open = descriptor_pre_input_open(&tx, &b)?;
000886     if b["route"]["module_artifact_id"] != artifact {
000887         return Err(Error::new(
000888             "ARTIFACT_MISMATCH",
000889             "module artifact differs from the reserved route",
000890         ));
000891     }
000892     let module_contract_negotiation = super::module_handshake::negotiate_hello(
000893         &tx,
000894         model::text(&b, "module_artifact_id")?,
000895         b["observation"].get("module_contract_selector"),
000896         v.get("module_contract"),
000897     )?;
000898     if verified.get("module_contract_negotiation") != Some(&module_contract_negotiation) {
000899         return Err(Error::new(
000900             "STALE_MODULE_CONTRACT",
000901             "trusted module descriptor changed during hello preflight",
000902         ));
000903     }
000904     let sessionless_batch = crate::runtime::batch::is_sessionless_route(&b["route"]);
000905     let requested_root = optional_identity_text(v, "native_root_id")?;
000906     let requested_scope = optional_identity_text(v, "native_scope_key")?;
000907     if requested_root.is_some() != requested_scope.is_some() {
000908         return Err(Error::new(
000909             "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000910             "native root and scope must be supplied together",
000911         ));
000912     }
000913     let retained_root = optional_identity_text(&b, "native_root_id")?;
000914     let retained_scope = optional_identity_text(&b, "native_scope_key")?;
000915     if retained_root.is_some() != retained_scope.is_some() {
000916         return Err(Error::new(
000917             "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000918             "Store retained an incomplete native identity pair",
000919         ));
000920     }
000921     if sessionless_batch && (requested_root.is_some() || retained_root.is_some()) {
000922         return Err(Error::new(
000923             "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000924             "sessionless batch bindings cannot claim a native root or scope",
000925         ));
000926     }
000927     match (
000928         retained_root,
000929         retained_scope,
000930         requested_root,
000931         requested_scope,
000932     ) {
000933         (
000934             Some(retained_root),
000935             Some(retained_scope),
000936             Some(requested_root),
000937             Some(requested_scope),
000938         ) if retained_root != requested_root || retained_scope != requested_scope => {
000939             return Err(Error::new(
000940                 "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000941                 "supplied native identity differs from the Store-owned pair",
000942             ));
000943         }
000944         (None, None, Some(_), Some(_)) => {
000945             return Err(Error::new(
000946                 "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000947                 "module hello cannot introduce a native identity that Store has not retained",
000948             ));
000949         }
000950         (Some(_), Some(_), None, None)
000951         | (Some(_), Some(_), Some(_), Some(_))
000952         | (None, None, None, None) => {}
000953         _ => {
000954             return Err(Error::new(
000955                 "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000956                 "native root and scope must be complete identity pairs",
000957             ));
000958         }
000959     }
000960     let old_boot = b["observation"]["bridge_boot_id"].as_str();
000961     if verified["old_boot"] != b["observation"]["bridge_boot_id"]
000962         || verified["owner"] != b["observation"]["managed_owner"]
000963     {
000964         return Err(Error::new(
000965             "STALE_RECOVERY",
000966             "module owner changed during OS inspection",
@@ 928:1089 @@
000928         retained_root,
000929         retained_scope,
000930         requested_root,
000931         requested_scope,
000932     ) {
000933         (
000934             Some(retained_root),
000935             Some(retained_scope),
000936             Some(requested_root),
000937             Some(requested_scope),
000938         ) if retained_root != requested_root || retained_scope != requested_scope => {
000939             return Err(Error::new(
000940                 "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000941                 "supplied native identity differs from the Store-owned pair",
000942             ));
000943         }
000944         (None, None, Some(_), Some(_)) => {
000945             return Err(Error::new(
000946                 "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000947                 "module hello cannot introduce a native identity that Store has not retained",
000948             ));
000949         }
000950         (Some(_), Some(_), None, None)
000951         | (Some(_), Some(_), Some(_), Some(_))
000952         | (None, None, None, None) => {}
000953         _ => {
000954             return Err(Error::new(
000955                 "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000956                 "native root and scope must be complete identity pairs",
000957             ));
000958         }
000959     }
000960     let old_boot = b["observation"]["bridge_boot_id"].as_str();
000961     if verified["old_boot"] != b["observation"]["bridge_boot_id"]
000962         || verified["owner"] != b["observation"]["managed_owner"]
000963     {
000964         return Err(Error::new(
000965             "STALE_RECOVERY",
000966             "module owner changed during OS inspection",
000967         ));
000968     }
000969     if old_boot == Some(boot) && v.get("managed_owner") != b["observation"].get("managed_owner") {
000970         return Err(Error::new(
000971             "OWNER_MISMATCH",
000972             "a live bridge cannot replace its process owner",
000973         ));
000974     }
000975     let recovered = old_boot.is_some_and(|old| old != boot) && verified["departed"] == true;
000976     let mut needs_recovery = false;
000977     if old_boot.is_some_and(|old| old != boot) {
000978         let possible: i64 = if sessionless_batch {
000979             tx.query_row("SELECT count(*) FROM operations WHERE binding_id=?1 AND binding_generation=?2 AND state IN ('sending','native_accepted','outcome_unknown') AND method IN ('agent.open','task.dispatch','agent.refresh','agent.reconcile','native.mcp.install','native.mcp.observe','native.mcp.arm','native.mcp.read')",params![id,generation],|r|r.get(0))?
000980         } else {
000981             tx.query_row("SELECT count(*) FROM operations WHERE binding_id=?1 AND binding_generation=?2 AND state IN ('sending','native_accepted','outcome_unknown','settled') AND method IN ('agent.open','task.dispatch','agent.send','agent.reply','agent.configure','agent.goal','native.mcp.install','native.mcp.observe','native.mcp.arm','native.mcp.read')",params![id,generation],|r|r.get(0))?
000982         };
000983         needs_recovery = possible > 0 || (!sessionless_batch && !b["native_root_id"].is_null());
000984         if needs_recovery && !recovered {
000985             return Err(Error::new(
000986                 "RECOVERY_REQUIRED",
000987                 "previous module may own native work; a new bridge must not spawn a second executor",
000988             ));
000989         }
000990     }
000991     if recovered && v.get("managed_owner").is_none() && !sessionless_batch {
000992         return Err(Error::new(
000993             "MANAGED_OWNER_REQUIRED",
000994             "recovery requires the non-killing module launcher",
000995         ));
000996     }
000997     if recovered && needs_recovery {
000998         tx.execute("UPDATE bindings SET state='reconciling',state_json=json_set(state_json,'$.recovery_required',json('true'),'$.previous_bridge_boot_id',?3) WHERE binding_id=?1 AND generation=?2", params![id,generation,old_boot])?;
000999         tx.execute("UPDATE operations SET state='outcome_unknown',updated_at_ms=?3 WHERE binding_id=?1 AND binding_generation=?2 AND state IN ('sending','native_accepted')",params![id,generation,model::now_ms()?])?;
001000         // The unknown outcomes free no capacity: the ledger marks the
001001         // entries unknown and keeps their phases (R23).
001002         super::capacity::sync_binding(&tx, &id, generation, model::now_ms()?)?;
001003     }
001004     if let Some(owner) = v.get("managed_owner") {
001005         tx.execute("UPDATE bindings SET state_json=json_set(state_json,'$.managed_owner',json(?3)) WHERE binding_id=?1 AND generation=?2",params![id,generation,model::canonical(owner)?])?;
001006     }
001007     let prepared_rootless_resume = if old_boot == Some(boot)
001008         && (pre_input_open.is_some()
001009             || crate::runtime::prepared::is_prepared_claude_route(&b["route"]))
001010         && b["native_root_id"].is_null()
001011         && b["native_scope_key"].is_null()
001012         && v["native_ready"] == true
001013         && b["observation"]["recovery_required"] != true
001014         && b["observation"]["opening_evidence"]["completion_condition"]
001015             == "native_executor_prepared"
001016         && b["observation"]["opening_evidence"]["native_session_state"] == "prepared"
001017         && b["observation"]["opening_evidence"]["bridge_boot_id"] == boot
001018     {
001019         !tx.query_row(
001020             "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM operations WHERE binding_id=?1 AND binding_generation=?2 AND state IN ('sending','native_accepted','outcome_unknown') AND method IN ('agent.open','task.dispatch'))",
001021             params![id, generation],
001022             |row| row.get::<_, bool>(0),
001023         )?
001024     } else {
001025         false
001026     };
001027     let reply_route = if standalone_opencode_route(&b["route"]) {
001028         module_route_projection(&tx, config, &b)?
001029     } else {
001030         b["route"].clone()
001031     };
001032     tx.execute("UPDATE bindings SET state_json=json_set(state_json,'$.bridge_boot_id',?3,'$.module_link_id',?4,'$.connection','connected','$.connected_at_ms',?5),state=CASE WHEN state='reconciling' AND ?6 AND COALESCE(json_extract(state_json,'$.recovery_required'),0)=0 AND (native_root_id IS NOT NULL OR ?7 OR ?8) THEN 'ready' ELSE state END WHERE binding_id=?1 AND generation=?2", params![id,generation,boot,p.link_id,model::now_ms()?,v["native_ready"]==true,sessionless_batch,prepared_rootless_resume])?;
001033     let startup_recovery = super::module_demand::startup_recovery_projection(
001034         &tx,
001035         &id,
001036         generation,
001037         &p.client_id,
001038         &p.link_id,
001039         boot,
001040     )?;
001041     let result = json!({"binding_id":id,"generation":generation,"route":reply_route,"host_epoch":meta(&tx,"host_epoch")?,"native_root_id":b["native_root_id"],"native_scope_key":b["native_scope_key"],"recovery_required":(recovered && needs_recovery) || b["observation"]["recovery_required"]==true,"module_contract_negotiation":module_contract_negotiation,"startup_recovery":startup_recovery});
001042     tx.commit()?;
001043     Ok(result)
001044 }
001045 
001046 fn optional_identity_text<'a>(value: &'a Value, field: &str) -> Result<Option<&'a str>> {
001047     match value.get(field) {
001048         None | Some(Value::Null) => Ok(None),
001049         Some(Value::String(value)) if !value.trim().is_empty() => Ok(Some(value)),
001050         _ => Err(Error::new(
001051             "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
001052             "native identity fields must be nonempty strings or absent",
001053         )),
001054     }
001055 }

001057 fn standalone_opencode_route(route: &Value) -> bool {
001058     route["runtime"] == "module"
001059         && route["module_artifact_id"] == crate::config::OPENCODE_RUST_ARTIFACT_ID

001063 /// standalone schema uses native connection fields and an optional invocation

001078                 "owned module projection requires the current controller configuration",
001079             )
001080         })?;
001081         let id = model::text(binding, "binding_id")?;
001082         let generation = model::positive(binding, "generation")?;

001084             super::launcher_owned_service::module_owned_native_options(

001041     let result = json!({"binding_id":id,"generation":generation,"route":reply_route,"host_epoch":meta(&tx,"host_epoch")?,"native_root_id":b["native_root_id"],"native_scope_key":b["native_scope_key"],"recovery_required":(recovered && needs_recovery) || b["observation"]["recovery_required"]==true,"module_contract_negotiation":module_contract_negotiation,"startup_recovery":startup_recovery});
001042     tx.commit()?;
001043     Ok(result)
001044 }

001047     match value.get(field) {
001048         None | Some(Value::Null) => Ok(None),
001049         Some(Value::String(value)) if !value.trim().is_empty() => Ok(Some(value)),
001050         _ => Err(Error::new(
001051             "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
001052             "native identity fields must be nonempty strings or absent",

001057 fn standalone_opencode_route(route: &Value) -> bool {
001058     route["runtime"] == "module"
001059         && route["module_artifact_id"] == crate::config::OPENCODE_RUST_ARTIFACT_ID
001060 }
001061 
001062 /// Hello and commands share the same authenticated route projection. The
001063 /// standalone schema uses native connection fields and an optional invocation
001064 /// model override. The built-in adapter's expected_version is not a field in
001065 /// that module contract. This changes only
001066 /// the reply, never the retained binding route or original Operation digest.
001067 fn module_route_projection(
001068     db: &Connection,
001069     config: Option<&crate::config::Config>,
001070     binding: &Value,
001071 ) -> Result<Value> {
001072     let mut route = binding["route"].clone();
001073     let standalone_opencode = standalone_opencode_route(&route);
001074     if route.get("owned_service").is_some_and(|v| !v.is_null()) {
001075         let config = config.ok_or_else(|| {
001076             Error::new(
001077                 "OWNED_SERVICE_SCOPE",
001078                 "owned module projection requires the current controller configuration",
001079             )
001080         })?;
001081         let id = model::text(binding, "binding_id")?;
001082         let generation = model::positive(binding, "generation")?;
001083         route["native_options"] = if standalone_opencode {
001084             super::launcher_owned_service::module_owned_native_options(
001085                 db,
001086                 config,
001087                 id,
001088                 generation,
001089                 route["native_options"].clone(),
001090             )?
001091         } else {
001092             json!(
001093                 super::launcher_owned_service::effective_options_for_binding(
001094                     db, config, id, generation,
001095                 )?
001096             )
001097         };
001098     }
001099     if standalone_opencode {
001100         let options = route["native_options"].as_object_mut().ok_or_else(|| {
001101             Error::new("CONFIG_ERROR", "OpenCode native options must be an object")
001102         })?;
001103         // Historical owned receipts may carry the built-in caller pin. It is
001104         // not part of this module's invocation contract or its stored route.
001105         options.remove("expected_version");
001106         crate::config::validate_opencode_rust_options(&route["native_options"])?;
001107     }
001108     Ok(route)
001109 }

001006     }

001008         && (pre_input_open.is_some()
001009             || crate::runtime::prepared::is_prepared_claude_route(&b["route"]))
001010         && b["native_root_id"].is_null()
001011         && b["native_scope_key"].is_null()
001012         && v["native_ready"] == true

001016         && b["observation"]["opening_evidence"]["native_session_state"] == "prepared"

001022             |row| row.get::<_, bool>(0),
001023         )?

001025         false
001026     };

001029     } else {

001042     tx.commit()?;
001043     Ok(result)

001123 pub(super) fn binding_route_admission(
001124     db: &Connection,
001125     binding: &Value,
001126     config: Option<&crate::config::Config>,
001127     exclude_operation_id: Option<&str>,
001128 ) -> Result<super::provider_conditions::RouteAdmissionProjection> {
001129     // Native identity stays bound to the retained route, including an admitted
001130     // workspace. Only the current enablement and optional root policy change.
001131     let mut route: crate::config::Route = serde_json::from_value(binding["route"].clone())?;
001132     if let Some(config) = config {
001133         let current = config
001134             .routes
001135             .iter()
001136             .find(|current| current.alias == route.alias);
001137         route.enabled = current.is_some_and(|current| current.enabled);
001138         route.admission_policy = current.and_then(|current| current.admission_policy.clone());
001139     }
001140     super::launcher::route_admission(db, &route, Some(binding), exclude_operation_id)
001141 }

001143 fn validate_command_session_control(
001144     db: &Connection,
001145     binding: &Value,
001146     method: &str,
001147     input: &Value,
001148 ) -> Result<()> {
001149     let id = model::text(binding, "binding_id")?;
001150     let generation = model::positive(binding, "generation")?;
001151     let session = model::text(input, "session_id")?;
001152     let target_id = model::text(input, "target_operation_id")?;
001153     let target = operations::get_operation(db, target_id)?;
001154     if binding["module_artifact_id"] != "eliot-command.acp-rust.1"
001155         || binding["native_root_id"] != session
001156         || target["binding_id"] != id
001157         || target["binding_generation"] != generation
001158         || !matches!(
001159             target["method"].as_str(),
001160             Some("agent.open" | "task.dispatch" | "agent.send")
001161         )
@@ 1609:1770 @@
001609             && b["route"]["runtime"] != crate::runtime::opencode_v2::RUNTIME
001610         {
001611             let target = operations::get_operation(&tx, model::text(&input, "operation_id")?)?;
001612             matches!(
001613                 target["method"].as_str(),
001614                 Some("task.dispatch" | "agent.send" | "agent.goal" | "agent.recover")
001615             )
001616         } else {
001617             false
001618         };
001619         let starts_work = reconcile_starts_work
001620             || method == "agent.open"
001621             || method == "agent.recover"
001622             || method == "task.dispatch"
001623             || (method == "agent.send" && input["delivery"] == "next_turn")
001624             || method == "native.opencode.loop_step"
001625             || (method == "agent.goal"
001626                 && matches!(
001627                     input["action"].as_str(),
001628                     Some("set" | "edit" | "resume" | "continue")
001629                 ));
001630         starts_native_work = starts_work;
001631         if starts_work
001632             && meta(&tx, "execution_mode")?.unwrap_or(Value::Null)["new_work"] != "enabled"
001633         {
001634             return Err(Error::new(
001635                 "ADMISSION_DISABLED",
001636                 "new work disabled before dispatch",
001637             ));
001638         }
001639         if method == "agent.recover"
001640             && (caller["role"] != "operator"
001641                 || input["expected_boot_id"] != b["observation"]["bridge_boot_id"]
001642                 || b["observation"]["recovery_required"] != true)
001643         {
001644             return Err(Error::new(
001645                 "STALE_RECOVERY",
001646                 "recovery must target this unresolved bridge boot",
001647             ));
001648         }
001649         if method == "agent.open" {
001650             if b["state"] != "opening" || !b["native_root_id"].is_null() {
001651                 return Err(Error::conflict("root already opened or changed"));
001652             }
001653             if b["route"]
001654                 .get("owned_service")
001655                 .is_some_and(|v| !v.is_null())
001656             {
001657                 let config = config.ok_or_else(|| {
001658                     Error::new(
001659                         "OWNED_SERVICE_SCOPE",
001660                         "owned open requires the current controller configuration",
001661                     )
001662                 })?;
001663                 if standalone_opencode_route(&b["route"]) {
001664                     super::launcher_owned_service::validate_module_owned_open_dispatch(
001665                         &tx, config, &id, generation, &op,
001666                     )?;
001667                 } else {
001668                     super::launcher_owned_service::validate_owned_open_dispatch(
001669                         &tx, config, &id, generation, &op,
001670                     )?;
001671                 }
001672             }
001673         } else {
001674             let rootless_open_reconcile =
001675                 allows_rootless_open_reconcile(&tx, &method, &input, &b, &id, generation)?;
001676             if !normalized_result_admitted
001677                 && b["state"] != "ready"
001678                 && !is_recovery_control(&method, &input, &b, rootless_open_reconcile)
001679             {
001680                 return Err(Error::new(
001681                     "BINDING_NOT_READY",
001682                     "binding not ready before dispatch",
001683                 ));
001684             }
001685         }
001686         if method != "agent.open"
001687             && repair_context.is_none()
001688             && goal_continuation_context.is_none()
001689             && !normalized_result_admitted
001690             && !internal_native_mcp
001691             && caller["role"] != "operator"
001692         {
001693             let caller_id = model::text(&o, "caller_id")?;
001694             let owns: bool = tx.query_row(
001695                 "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM attempts WHERE owner_id=?1 AND binding_id=?2 AND binding_generation=?3 AND released_at_ms IS NULL)",
001696                 params![caller_id, id, generation],
001697                 |r| r.get(0),
001698             )?;
001699             if !owns {
001700                 if caller["role"] != "manager" {
001701                     return Err(Error::new(
001702                         "FORBIDDEN",
001703                         "original caller no longer has an assignment here",
001704                     ));
001705                 }
001706                 let attempt_id = if method == "task.dispatch" {
001707                     model::text(&input, "attempt_id")?
001708                 } else {
001709                     model::text(&o, "attempt_id")?
001710                 };
001711                 let attempt = tasks::get_attempt(&tx, attempt_id)?;
001712                 if attempt["binding_id"] != id || attempt["binding_generation"] != generation {
001713                     return Err(Error::new(
001714                         "FORBIDDEN",
001715                         "queued Operation Attempt belongs to another binding generation",
001716                     ));

003557             return Err(Error::conflict("observation ID reused for different state"));
003558         }
003559         return Ok(json!({"recorded":true,"replayed":true}));
003560     }

003527 pub(super) fn observe(db: &mut Connection, p: &Principal, v: &Value) -> Result<Value> {
003528     model::fields(v, &["event_id", "sequence", "state"])?;
003529     let event = model::text(v, "event_id")?;
003530     let sequence = v
003531         .get("sequence")
003532         .map(|n| {
003533             n.as_i64()
003534                 .filter(|n| *n >= 0)
003535                 .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("sequence must be a nonnegative integer"))
003536         })
003537         .transpose()?;
003538     if !v["state"].is_object() {
003539         return Err(Error::invalid(
003540             "state must be a compact native observation object",
003541         ));
003542     }
003543     let tx = db.transaction_with_behavior(TransactionBehavior::Immediate)?;
003544     let (id, generation, b) = scope(&tx, p, true)?;
003545     if v["state"]["boot_id"] != b["observation"]["bridge_boot_id"] {
003546         return Err(Error::new(
003547             "STALE_BOOT",
003548             "observation is not from the active module boot",
003549         ));
003550     }
003551     let usage = super::native_usage::parse(&v["state"], &b, sequence)?;
003552     let now = model::now_ms()?;
003553     let encoded = model::canonical(&v["state"])?;
003554     let previous:Option<String> = tx.query_row("SELECT payload_json FROM observations WHERE source_stream_id=?1 AND source_event_key=?2",params![format!("module:{}",p.client_id),event],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
003555     if let Some(previous) = previous {
003556         if previous != encoded {
003557             return Err(Error::conflict("observation ID reused for different state"));
003558         }
003559         return Ok(json!({"recorded":true,"replayed":true}));
003560     }
003561     let prior = &b["observation"]["native"];
003562     let last_sequence = b["observation"]["native_sequence"].as_i64();
003563     // Existing live modules may omit the additive sequence field. They retain
003564     // their legacy partial observation path, never overwrite an ordered stream,
003565     // and do not acquire the newer monotonic-projection guarantee.
003566     let current = prior["boot_id"] != v["state"]["boot_id"]
003567         || match (sequence, last_sequence) {
003568             (Some(n), Some(last)) => n > last,
003569             (None, Some(_)) => false,
003570             (_, None) => true,
003571         };
003572     tx.execute("INSERT INTO observations(source_stream_id,source_event_key,binding_id,binding_generation,kind,payload_json,recorded_at_ms) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,'runtime.state',?5,?6)",params![format!("module:{}",p.client_id),event,id,generation,encoded,now])?;
003573     let observation_id = tx.last_insert_rowid();
003574     if !current {
003575         tx.commit()?;
003576         return Ok(json!({"recorded":true,"stale":true}));
003577     }
003578     let observed_configurations =
003579         prerequisites::observed_configurations(&b, &v["state"], observation_id, now)?;
003580     tx.execute("UPDATE bindings SET state_json=json_set(state_json,'$.native',json(?3),'$.observed_at_ms',?4,'$.native_sequence',?5,'$.native_observation_id',?6) WHERE binding_id=?1 AND generation=?2",params![id,generation,encoded,now,sequence,observation_id])?;
003581     if let Some(usage) = usage.as_ref() {
003582         super::native_usage::retain(&tx, &b, usage, observation_id)?;
003583         super::provider_conditions::observe_usage(
003584             &tx,
003585             &b,
003586             usage,
003587             sequence.ok_or_else(|| {
003588                 Error::new(
003589                     "USAGE_SEQUENCE_REQUIRED",
003590                     "provider facts require ordered observations",
003591                 )
003592             })?,
003593             observation_id,
003594         )?;
003595         // New evidence wakes the exact held operations to re-evaluate the
003596         // predicate. It does not authorize input or claim provider recovery.
003597         tx.execute(
003598             "UPDATE operations SET due_at_ms=?3 WHERE binding_id=?1 AND binding_generation=?2 \
003599              AND state='queued' AND due_at_ms>?3 \
003600              AND json_extract(result_json,'$.route_admission.decision')='hold'",
003601             params![id, generation, now],
003602         )?;
003603     }
003604     for configuration in observed_configurations {
003605         tx.execute(
003606             &format!(
003607                 "UPDATE bindings SET state_json=json_set(state_json,'{}',json(?3)) WHERE binding_id=?1 AND generation=?2",
003608                 prerequisites::slot_path(configuration.slot)
003609             ),
003610             params![id, generation, model::canonical(&configuration.value)?],
003611         )?;
003612     }
003613     if v["state"]["turns"].is_array()
003614         || v["state"]["observed_children"].is_array()
003615         || v["state"]["input_executions"].is_array()
003616     {
003617         let mut stmt = tx.prepare("SELECT attempt_id, producers_json FROM attempts WHERE binding_id=?1 AND binding_generation=?2 AND released_at_ms IS NULL")?;
003618         let rows = stmt
003619             .query_map(params![id, generation], |r| {
003620                 Ok((r.get::<_, String>(0)?, r.get::<_, String>(1)?))
003621             })?
003622             .collect::<rusqlite::Result<Vec<_>>>()?;
003623         drop(stmt);
003624         for (attempt, raw) in &rows {
003625             let mut producers: Vec<Value> = serde_json::from_str(raw)?;
003626             for producer in &mut producers {
003627                 producers::apply_evidence(producer, &v["state"], Some(observation_id));
003628             }
003629             tx.execute(
003630                 "UPDATE attempts SET producers_json=?2 WHERE attempt_id=?1",
003631                 params![attempt, model::canonical(&json!(producers))?],
003632             )?;
003633         }
003634         for (attempt, _) in &rows {
003635             super::capacity::sync_attempt(&tx, attempt, now)?;
003636         }
003637     }
003638     tx.commit()?;
003639     Ok(json!({"recorded":true,"stale":false,"observation_id":observation_id}))
003640 }

005220 fn user_command_with_actor(
005221     tx: &Connection,
005222     actor: UserCommandActor<'_>,
005223     method: &str,
005224     v: &Value,
005225     op: &str,
005226     config: &crate::config::Config,
005227 ) -> Result<Value> {
005228     // Envelope shape is validated before persistence by model::validate_mutation.
005229     let id = model::text(v, "binding_id")?;
005230     let generation = model::positive(v, "generation")?;
005231     let b = operations::get_binding(tx, id, generation)?;
005232     let generic_result_selector =
005233         method == "agent.result" && super::normalized_result::uses_generic_selector(&v["selector"]);
005234     let normalized_result_request =
005235         generic_result_selector && super::normalized_result::enabled(tx, &b)?;
005236     if generic_result_selector && !normalized_result_request {
005237         return Err(Error::new(
005238             "RESULT_SELECTOR_UNSUPPORTED",
005239             "result selector requires the descriptor-admitted normalized result contract",
005240         ));
005241     }
005242     let command_result_route =
005243         method == "agent.result" && crate::runtime::batch::is_rust_command_route(&b["route"]);
005244     let strict_command_result = command_result_route && !normalized_result_request;
005245     let command_capture_release =
005246         method == swarm_contracts::module_command::COMMAND_RELEASE_EVIDENCE_METHOD;
005247     let command_capture_release_target = if command_capture_release {
005248         validate_command_capture_release_request(tx, &b, v, config)?;
005249         Some(operations::get_operation(
005250             tx,
005251             model::text(v, "target_operation_id")?,
005252         )?)
005253     } else {
005254         None
005255     };
005256     let command_result_target_snapshot = if command_result_route
005257         && matches!(
005258             v["selector"]["kind"].as_str(),
005259             Some("command_status" | "command_output")
005260         ) {
005261         Some(super::command_results::validate_request(tx, &b, v)?)
005262     } else {
005263         None
005264     };
005265     if crate::runtime::batch::is_sessionless_route(&b["route"])
005266         && !strict_command_result
005267         && !normalized_result_request
005268         && !command_capture_release
005269     {
005270         crate::runtime::batch::validate_command(&b["route"], method, v)?;
005271     }
005272     let normalized_target_attempt_id = if normalized_result_request {
005273         let target_id = model::text(&v["selector"], "input_operation_id")?;
005274         let target = operations::get_operation(tx, target_id)?;
005275         if target["method"] != "task.dispatch"
005276             || target["binding_id"] != id
005277             || target["binding_generation"] != generation
005278         {
005279             return Err(Error::new(
005280                 "RESULT_ORIGIN_INVALID",
005281                 "normalized result selector must name a task.dispatch on this binding generation",
@@ 5436:5597 @@
005436                     ));
005437                 }
005438             }
005439             UserCommandActor::Direct(principal) if principal.role == Role::Manager => {
005440                 if manager_attempt
005441                     .as_ref()
005442                     .is_none_or(|selected| selected["attempt_id"] != attempt["attempt_id"])
005443                 {
005444                     return Err(Error::new(
005445                         "FORBIDDEN",
005446                         "Manager authority was not established for the exact result Attempt",
005447                     ));
005448                 }
005449             }
005450             UserCommandActor::Direct(principal) if principal.role == Role::Operator => {}
005451             _ => {

001046 fn optional_identity_text<'a>(value: &'a Value, field: &str) -> Result<Option<&'a str>> {
001047     match value.get(field) {
001048         None | Some(Value::Null) => Ok(None),
001049         Some(Value::String(value)) if !value.trim().is_empty() => Ok(Some(value)),
001050         _ => Err(Error::new(
001051             "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
001052             "native identity fields must be nonempty strings or absent",
001053         )),
001054     }
001055 }

001057 fn standalone_opencode_route(route: &Value) -> bool {
001058     route["runtime"] == "module"
001059         && route["module_artifact_id"] == crate::config::OPENCODE_RUST_ARTIFACT_ID
001060 }
001061 
001062 /// Hello and commands share the same authenticated route projection. The
001063 /// standalone schema uses native connection fields and an optional invocation
001064 /// model override. The built-in adapter's expected_version is not a field in
001065 /// that module contract. This changes only
001066 /// the reply, never the retained binding route or original Operation digest.
001067 fn module_route_projection(
001068     db: &Connection,
001069     config: Option<&crate::config::Config>,

001075         let config = config.ok_or_else(|| {
001076             Error::new(
001077                 "OWNED_SERVICE_SCOPE",
001078                 "owned module projection requires the current controller configuration",
001079             )
001080         })?;
001081         let id = model::text(binding, "binding_id")?;
001082         let generation = model::positive(binding, "generation")?;
001083         route["native_options"] = if standalone_opencode {
001084             super::launcher_owned_service::module_owned_native_options(
001085                 db,
001086                 config,
001087                 id,
001088                 generation,
001089                 route["native_options"].clone(),
001090             )?
001091         } else {
001092             json!(
001093                 super::launcher_owned_service::effective_options_for_binding(
001094                     db, config, id, generation,
001095                 )?
001096             )
001097         };
001098     }

000951         | (Some(_), Some(_), Some(_), Some(_))
000952         | (None, None, None, None) => {}

000954             return Err(Error::new(
000955                 "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000956                 "native root and scope must be complete identity pairs",
