000977     if old_boot.is_some_and(|old| old != boot) {
000978         let possible: i64 = if sessionless_batch {
000979             tx.query_row("SELECT count(*) FROM operations WHERE binding_id=?1 AND binding_generation=?2 AND state IN ('sending','native_accepted','outcome_unknown') AND method IN ('agent.open','task.dispatch','agent.refresh','agent.reconcile','native.mcp.install','native.mcp.observe','native.mcp.arm','native.mcp.read')",params![id,generation],|r|r.get(0))?
000980         } else {
000981             tx.query_row("SELECT count(*) FROM operations WHERE binding_id=?1 AND binding_generation=?2 AND state IN ('sending','native_accepted','outcome_unknown','settled') AND method IN ('agent.open','task.dispatch','agent.send','agent.reply','agent.configure','agent.goal','native.mcp.install','native.mcp.observe','native.mcp.arm','native.mcp.read')",params![id,generation],|r|r.get(0))?
000982         };
000983         needs_recovery = possible > 0 || (!sessionless_batch && !b["native_root_id"].is_null());
000984         if needs_recovery && !recovered {
000985             return Err(Error::new(
000986                 "RECOVERY_REQUIRED",
000987                 "previous module may own native work; a new bridge must not spawn a second executor",
000988             ));
000989         }
000990     }
000991     if recovered && v.get("managed_owner").is_none() && !sessionless_batch {
000992         return Err(Error::new(
000993             "MANAGED_OWNER_REQUIRED",
000994             "recovery requires the non-killing module launcher",
000995         ));
000996     }
000997     if recovered && needs_recovery {
000998         tx.execute("UPDATE bindings SET state='reconciling',state_json=json_set(state_json,'$.recovery_required',json('true'),'$.previous_bridge_boot_id',?3) WHERE binding_id=?1 AND generation=?2", params![id,generation,old_boot])?;
000999         tx.execute("UPDATE operations SET state='outcome_unknown',updated_at_ms=?3 WHERE binding_id=?1 AND binding_generation=?2 AND state IN ('sending','native_accepted')",params![id,generation,model::now_ms()?])?;
001000         // The unknown outcomes free no capacity: the ledger marks the
001001         // entries unknown and keeps their phases (R23).
001002         super::capacity::sync_binding(&tx, &id, generation, model::now_ms()?)?;
001003     }
001004     if let Some(owner) = v.get("managed_owner") {
001005         tx.execute("UPDATE bindings SET state_json=json_set(state_json,'$.managed_owner',json(?3)) WHERE binding_id=?1 AND generation=?2",params![id,generation,model::canonical(owner)?])?;
001006     }
001007     let prepared_rootless_resume = if old_boot == Some(boot)
001008         && (pre_input_open.is_some()
001009             || crate::runtime::prepared::is_prepared_claude_route(&b["route"]))
001010         && b["native_root_id"].is_null()
001011         && b["native_scope_key"].is_null()
001012         && v["native_ready"] == true
001013         && b["observation"]["recovery_required"] != true
001014         && b["observation"]["opening_evidence"]["completion_condition"]
001015             == "native_executor_prepared"
001016         && b["observation"]["opening_evidence"]["native_session_state"] == "prepared"
001017         && b["observation"]["opening_evidence"]["bridge_boot_id"] == boot
001018     {
001019         !tx.query_row(
001020             "SELECT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM operations WHERE binding_id=?1 AND binding_generation=?2 AND state IN ('sending','native_accepted','outcome_unknown') AND method IN ('agent.open','task.dispatch'))",
001021             params![id, generation],
001022             |row| row.get::<_, bool>(0),
001023         )?
001024     } else {
001025         false
001026     };
001027     let reply_route = if standalone_opencode_route(&b["route"]) {
001028         module_route_projection(&tx, config, &b)?
001029     } else {
001030         b["route"].clone()
001031     };
001032     tx.execute("UPDATE bindings SET state_json=json_set(state_json,'$.bridge_boot_id',?3,'$.module_link_id',?4,'$.connection','connected','$.connected_at_ms',?5),state=CASE WHEN state='reconciling' AND ?6 AND COALESCE(json_extract(state_json,'$.recovery_required'),0)=0 AND (native_root_id IS NOT NULL OR ?7 OR ?8) THEN 'ready' ELSE state END WHERE binding_id=?1 AND generation=?2", params![id,generation,boot,p.link_id,model::now_ms()?,v["native_ready"]==true,sessionless_batch,prepared_rootless_resume])?;
001033     let startup_recovery = super::module_demand::startup_recovery_projection(
001034         &tx,
001035         &id,
001036         generation,
001037         &p.client_id,
001038         &p.link_id,
001039         boot,
001040     )?;
001041     let result = json!({"binding_id":id,"generation":generation,"route":reply_route,"host_epoch":meta(&tx,"host_epoch")?,"native_root_id":b["native_root_id"],"native_scope_key":b["native_scope_key"],"recovery_required":(recovered && needs_recovery) || b["observation"]["recovery_required"]==true,"module_contract_negotiation":module_contract_negotiation,"startup_recovery":startup_recovery});
001042     tx.commit()?;
001043     Ok(result)
001044 }
001045 
001046 fn optional_identity_text<'a>(value: &'a Value, field: &str) -> Result<Option<&'a str>> {
001047     match value.get(field) {
001048         None | Some(Value::Null) => Ok(None),
001049         Some(Value::String(value)) if !value.trim().is_empty() => Ok(Some(value)),
001050         _ => Err(Error::new(
001051             "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
001052             "native identity fields must be nonempty strings or absent",
001053         )),
001054     }
001055 }

001057 fn standalone_opencode_route(route: &Value) -> bool {
001058     route["runtime"] == "module"
001059         && route["module_artifact_id"] == crate::config::OPENCODE_RUST_ARTIFACT_ID
001060 }
001061 
001062 /// Hello and commands share the same authenticated route projection. The
001063 /// standalone schema uses native connection fields and an optional invocation
001064 /// model override. The built-in adapter's expected_version is not a field in
001065 /// that module contract. This changes only
001066 /// the reply, never the retained binding route or original Operation digest.
001067 fn module_route_projection(
001068     db: &Connection,
001069     config: Option<&crate::config::Config>,
001070     binding: &Value,
001071 ) -> Result<Value> {
001072     let mut route = binding["route"].clone();
001073     let standalone_opencode = standalone_opencode_route(&route);
001074     if route.get("owned_service").is_some_and(|v| !v.is_null()) {
001075         let config = config.ok_or_else(|| {
001076             Error::new(
001077                 "OWNED_SERVICE_SCOPE",
001078                 "owned module projection requires the current controller configuration",
001079             )
001080         })?;
001081         let id = model::text(binding, "binding_id")?;
001082         let generation = model::positive(binding, "generation")?;
001083         route["native_options"] = if standalone_opencode {
001084             super::launcher_owned_service::module_owned_native_options(
001085                 db,
001086                 config,
001087                 id,
001088                 generation,
001089                 route["native_options"].clone(),
001090             )?
001091         } else {
001092             json!(
001093                 super::launcher_owned_service::effective_options_for_binding(
001094                     db, config, id, generation,
001095                 )?
001096             )
001097         };
001098     }
001099     if standalone_opencode {
001100         let options = route["native_options"].as_object_mut().ok_or_else(|| {
001101             Error::new("CONFIG_ERROR", "OpenCode native options must be an object")
001102         })?;
001103         // Historical owned receipts may carry the built-in caller pin. It is
001104         // not part of this module's invocation contract or its stored route.
@@ 945:1106 @@
000945             return Err(Error::new(
000946                 "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000947                 "module hello cannot introduce a native identity that Store has not retained",
000948             ));
000949         }
000950         (Some(_), Some(_), None, None)
000951         | (Some(_), Some(_), Some(_), Some(_))
000952         | (None, None, None, None) => {}
000953         _ => {
000954             return Err(Error::new(
000955                 "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000956                 "native root and scope must be complete identity pairs",
000957             ));
000958         }
000959     }
000960     let old_boot = b["observation"]["bridge_boot_id"].as_str();
000961     if verified["old_boot"] != b["observation"]["bridge_boot_id"]
000962         || verified["owner"] != b["observation"]["managed_owner"]
000963     {
000964         return Err(Error::new(
000965             "STALE_RECOVERY",
000966             "module owner changed during OS inspection",
000967         ));
000968     }
000969     if old_boot == Some(boot) && v.get("managed_owner") != b["observation"].get("managed_owner") {
000970         return Err(Error::new(
000971             "OWNER_MISMATCH",
000972             "a live bridge cannot replace its process owner",
000973         ));
000974     }
000975     let recovered = old_boot.is_some_and(|old| old != boot) && verified["departed"] == true;
000976     let mut needs_recovery = false;
000977     if old_boot.is_some_and(|old| old != boot) {
000978         let possible: i64 = if sessionless_batch {

000980         } else {

000982         };

001046 fn optional_identity_text<'a>(value: &'a Value, field: &str) -> Result<Option<&'a str>> {

001052             "native identity fields must be nonempty strings or absent",

001057 fn standalone_opencode_route(route: &Value) -> bool {
001058     route["runtime"] == "module"
001059         && route["module_artifact_id"] == crate::config::OPENCODE_RUST_ARTIFACT_ID
001060 }
001061 
001062 /// Hello and commands share the same authenticated route projection. The
001063 /// standalone schema uses native connection fields and an optional invocation
001064 /// model override. The built-in adapter's expected_version is not a field in
001065 /// that module contract. This changes only
001066 /// the reply, never the retained binding route or original Operation digest.
001067 fn module_route_projection(
001068     db: &Connection,
001069     config: Option<&crate::config::Config>,
001070     binding: &Value,
001071 ) -> Result<Value> {
001072     let mut route = binding["route"].clone();
001073     let standalone_opencode = standalone_opencode_route(&route);
001074     if route.get("owned_service").is_some_and(|v| !v.is_null()) {
001075         let config = config.ok_or_else(|| {
001076             Error::new(
001077                 "OWNED_SERVICE_SCOPE",
001078                 "owned module projection requires the current controller configuration",
001079             )
001080         })?;
001081         let id = model::text(binding, "binding_id")?;
001082         let generation = model::positive(binding, "generation")?;
001083         route["native_options"] = if standalone_opencode {
001084             super::launcher_owned_service::module_owned_native_options(
001085                 db,
001086                 config,
001087                 id,
001088                 generation,
001089                 route["native_options"].clone(),
001090             )?
001091         } else {
001092             json!(
001093                 super::launcher_owned_service::effective_options_for_binding(
001094                     db, config, id, generation,
001095                 )?
001096             )
001097         };
001098     }
001099     if standalone_opencode {
001100         let options = route["native_options"].as_object_mut().ok_or_else(|| {
001101             Error::new("CONFIG_ERROR", "OpenCode native options must be an object")
001102         })?;
001103         // Historical owned receipts may carry the built-in caller pin. It is
001104         // not part of this module's invocation contract or its stored route.
001105         options.remove("expected_version");
001106         crate::config::validate_opencode_rust_options(&route["native_options"])?;
001107     }
001108     Ok(route)
001109 }

001143 fn validate_command_session_control(
001144     db: &Connection,
001145     binding: &Value,
001146     method: &str,
001147     input: &Value,
001148 ) -> Result<()> {
001149     let id = model::text(binding, "binding_id")?;
001150     let generation = model::positive(binding, "generation")?;
001151     let session = model::text(input, "session_id")?;
001152     let target_id = model::text(input, "target_operation_id")?;
001153     let target = operations::get_operation(db, target_id)?;
001154     if binding["module_artifact_id"] != "eliot-command.acp-rust.1"
001155         || binding["native_root_id"] != session
001156         || target["binding_id"] != id
001157         || target["binding_generation"] != generation
001158         || !matches!(
001159             target["method"].as_str(),
001160             Some("agent.open" | "task.dispatch" | "agent.send")
001161         )
001162     {
001163         return Err(Error::new(
001164             "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
001165             "Command control target belongs to another native session",
001166         ));
001167     }
001168     if method == "native.command.cancel_turn"
001169         && (input["turn_id"] != target_id
001170             || !matches!(

001302             let (scoped_id, scoped_generation, binding) =
001303                 admitted_result_scope(&tx, p, &operation_id)?;
001304             (scoped_id, scoped_generation, binding, true)
001305         }
001306         Err(error) => return Err(error),
001307     };
001308     let pre_input_open = if admitted_result_only {
001309         None
001310     } else {
001311         descriptor_pre_input_open(&tx, &b)?
001312     };
001313     if crate::runtime::batch::is_legacy_command_route(&b["route"]) {
001314         // Artifact .2 is retained for historical operation reads only. Never
001315         // hand queued work to an old bridge after the .3 receipt contract ships.
001316         return Ok(json!({"command":null,"reason":"command_artifact_retired"}));
001317     }
001318     if !admitted_result_only
001319         && !matches!(
001320             b["state"].as_str(),
001321             Some("opening" | "ready" | "reconciling")
001322         )
001323     {
001324         return Ok(json!({"command":null}));
001325     }
001326     // Readback, replies, continuation-stop controls, and exact-scope resource
001327     // shutdowns stay available while an ordinary mutation awaits application.
001328     // These controls do not admit a competing model input.

001330         let row:Option<(String,String,String,i64)>=tx.query_row(
001331             "SELECT operation_id,method,original_request_json,created_at_ms FROM operations AS candidate
001332              WHERE binding_id=?1 AND binding_generation=?2 AND state='queued' AND due_at_ms<=?3
001333                AND (?5=0 OR (method='agent.result' AND json_type(candidate.effective_request_json,'$.normalized_result_origin')='object'))
001334                AND (COALESCE(json_extract(?4,'$.recovery_required'),0)=0 OR method IN ('agent.recover','agent.reconcile'))
001335                AND method IN ('agent.open','task.dispatch','agent.send','agent.reply','agent.configure','agent.goal','agent.background','agent.refresh','agent.reconcile','agent.result','agent.recover','agent.shutdown','native.opencode.loop_step','native.command.cancel_turn','native.command.close_session','native.command.release_evidence','native.mcp.install','native.mcp.observe','native.mcp.arm','native.mcp.read')
001336                AND (method IN ('agent.reply','agent.background','agent.shutdown','agent.refresh','agent.reconcile','agent.result','agent.recover','native.opencode.loop_step','native.command.cancel_turn','native.command.close_session','native.command.release_evidence','native.mcp.install','native.mcp.observe','native.mcp.arm','native.mcp.read')
001337                  OR (method='agent.send' AND json_extract(original_request_json,'$.delivery')='steer')
001338                  OR (method='agent.goal' AND json_extract(original_request_json,'$.action') IN ('pause','clear'))
001339                  OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM operations AS pending
001340                    WHERE pending.binding_id=?1 AND pending.binding_generation=?2
001341                      AND pending.state IN ('sending','native_accepted','outcome_unknown')
001342                       AND pending.method IN ('agent.open','task.dispatch','agent.send','agent.configure','agent.goal','agent.recover','native.mcp.install','native.mcp.observe','native.mcp.arm','native.mcp.read')))
001343              ORDER BY CASE WHEN method IN ('native.mcp.install','native.mcp.observe','native.mcp.arm','native.mcp.read') THEN 0
001344                            WHEN method IN ('agent.reply','agent.background','agent.shutdown','native.opencode.loop_step','native.command.cancel_turn','native.command.close_session') THEN 1
001345                            WHEN method='agent.send' AND json_extract(original_request_json,'$.delivery')='steer' THEN 1
001346                            WHEN method='agent.goal' AND json_extract(original_request_json,'$.action') IN ('pause','clear') THEN 1
001347                             WHEN method IN ('agent.refresh','agent.reconcile','agent.result','agent.recover','native.command.release_evidence') THEN 2 ELSE 3 END, due_at_ms, rowid LIMIT 1",
001348             params![id,generation,model::now_ms()?,model::canonical(&b["observation"])?,admitted_result_only],|r|Ok((r.get(0)?,r.get(1)?,r.get(2)?,r.get(3)?))).optional()?;
001349         let Some((op, method, raw, created)) = row else {
001350             return Ok(json!({"command":null}));
001351         };
001352         let opening_actor = if method == "agent.open" {
001353             match super::launcher::opening_actor_for_open(&tx, &op, &id, generation) {
001354                 Ok(actor) => actor,
001355                 Err(error)
001356                     if matches!(
001357                         error.code.as_str(),
001358                         "STORE_ERROR" | "STORE_CLOSED" | "IO_ERROR" | "CLOCK_ERROR"
001359                     ) =>
001360                 {
001361                     return Err(error);
001362                 }
001363                 Err(error) => {
001364                     // This validation precedes the ordinary dispatch guard.
001365                     // Retain a deterministic rejection instead of rolling back
001366                     // to queued work that the executor silently retries forever.
001367                     let now = model::now_ms()?;
001368                     let safe_error = Error::new(
001369                         error.code,
001370                         "opening launch validation failed before dispatch",
001371                     );
001372                     let changed = tx.execute(
001373                         "UPDATE operations SET state='rejected',result_json=?2,settled_at_ms=?3,updated_at_ms=?3 WHERE operation_id=?1 AND state='queued'",
001374                         params![op, model::canonical(&json!(safe_error))?, now],
001375                     )?;
001376                     if changed != 1 {
001377                         return Err(Error::conflict(
001378                             "opening operation changed before validation rejection",
001379                         ));
001380                     }
001381                     operations::record_owned_open_dispatch_failure(
001382                         &tx,
001383                         &id,
001384                         generation,
001385                         "opening_actor_validate",
001386                         "rejected_before_dispatch",
001387                         &safe_error.code,
001388                         "rejected",
001389                     )?;
001390                     tx.commit()?;
001391                     return Ok(json!({
001392                         "command":null,
001393                         "rejected_operation_id":op,
001394                         "error":safe_error
001395                     }));
001396                 }
001397             }
001398         } else {
001399             None
001400         };
001401         let prerequisite = if opening_actor.is_some() {
001402             prerequisites::Gate::None
001403         } else {
001404             prerequisites::for_operation(&tx, &b, &op)?
001405         };
001406         match prerequisite {
001407             prerequisites::Gate::None | prerequisites::Gate::Ready { .. } => {
001408                 (op, method, raw, created, opening_actor)
001409             }
001410             prerequisites::Gate::Pending {
001411                 operation_id,
001412                 blocking_operation_id,
001413                 ..
001414             } => {
001415                 return Ok(json!({
001416                     "command":null,
001417                     "waiting_operation_id":op,
001418                     "waiting_for_prerequisite_operation_id":operation_id,
001419                     "blocking_configuration_operation_id":blocking_operation_id
001420                 }));
001421             }
001422             prerequisites::Gate::Failed(error) => {
001423                 let now = model::now_ms()?;
001424                 let result = json!({
001425                     "error":error,
001426                     "reason":"prerequisite_unsatisfied"
001427                 });
001428                 let changed = tx.execute(
001429                     "UPDATE operations SET state='rejected',result_json=?2,settled_at_ms=?3,updated_at_ms=?3 WHERE operation_id=?1 AND state='queued'",
001430                     params![op, model::canonical(&result)?, now],
001431                 )?;

003489             EffectOutcome::Accepted | EffectOutcome::Unknown => unreachable!(),
003490         };
003491         let occurrence_id = format!("operation:{}:{}", r.operation_id, phase);
003492         // A deterministic result rejection already has an authenticated,
003493         // retained Operation receipt. Carry only the closed result-diagnostic
003494         // vocabulary into the Manager event projection; transport uncertainty
003495         // remains the existing outcome_unknown path.
003496         let result_failure_code =
003497             if o["method"] == "agent.result" && matches!(r.outcome, EffectOutcome::Rejected) {
003498                 r.details["diagnostic_code"]
003499                     .as_str()
003500                     .filter(|code| super::is_safe_native_result_error_code(code))
003501             } else {
003502                 None
003503             };
003504         super::insert_safe_system_event(
003505             &tx,
003506             "controller:runtime",
003507             &format!("terminal:{}", r.operation_id),
003508             Some(&r.operation_id),
003509             "native.operation.completed",
003510             phase,
003511             status,
003512             Some(&occurrence_id),
003513             None,
003514             result_failure_code,
003515             now,
003516         )?;
003517     }
003518     super::capacity::note_outcome(&tx, &o, &r, now)?;
003519     super::capacity::sync_operation(&tx, &r.operation_id, now)?;
003520     if let Some(attempt_id) = o["attempt_id"].as_str() {
003521         super::capacity::sync_attempt(&tx, attempt_id, now)?;
003522     }
003523     tx.commit()?;
003524     Ok(json!({"recorded":true}))
003525 }

003527 pub(super) fn observe(db: &mut Connection, p: &Principal, v: &Value) -> Result<Value> {
003528     model::fields(v, &["event_id", "sequence", "state"])?;
003529     let event = model::text(v, "event_id")?;
003530     let sequence = v
003531         .get("sequence")
003532         .map(|n| {
003533             n.as_i64()
003534                 .filter(|n| *n >= 0)
003535                 .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("sequence must be a nonnegative integer"))
003536         })
003537         .transpose()?;
003538     if !v["state"].is_object() {
003539         return Err(Error::invalid(
003540             "state must be a compact native observation object",
003541         ));
003542     }

003544     let (id, generation, b) = scope(&tx, p, true)?;
003545     if v["state"]["boot_id"] != b["observation"]["bridge_boot_id"] {
003546         return Err(Error::new(
003547             "STALE_BOOT",
003548             "observation is not from the active module boot",
003549         ));
003550     }
003551     let usage = super::native_usage::parse(&v["state"], &b, sequence)?;
003552     let now = model::now_ms()?;
003553     let encoded = model::canonical(&v["state"])?;
003554     let previous:Option<String> = tx.query_row("SELECT payload_json FROM observations WHERE source_stream_id=?1 AND source_event_key=?2",params![format!("module:{}",p.client_id),event],|r|r.get(0)).optional()?;
003555     if let Some(previous) = previous {
003556         if previous != encoded {
003557             return Err(Error::conflict("observation ID reused for different state"));
003558         }
003559         return Ok(json!({"recorded":true,"replayed":true}));
003560     }
003561     let prior = &b["observation"]["native"];
003562     let last_sequence = b["observation"]["native_sequence"].as_i64();
003563     // Existing live modules may omit the additive sequence field. They retain
003564     // their legacy partial observation path, never overwrite an ordered stream,
003565     // and do not acquire the newer monotonic-projection guarantee.
003566     let current = prior["boot_id"] != v["state"]["boot_id"]
003567         || match (sequence, last_sequence) {
003568             (Some(n), Some(last)) => n > last,
003569             (None, Some(_)) => false,
003570             (_, None) => true,
003571         };
003572     tx.execute("INSERT INTO observations(source_stream_id,source_event_key,binding_id,binding_generation,kind,payload_json,recorded_at_ms) VALUES(?1,?2,?3,?4,'runtime.state',?5,?6)",params![format!("module:{}",p.client_id),event,id,generation,encoded,now])?;
003573     let observation_id = tx.last_insert_rowid();
003574     if !current {
003575         tx.commit()?;
003576         return Ok(json!({"recorded":true,"stale":true}));
003577     }
003578     let observed_configurations =
003579         prerequisites::observed_configurations(&b, &v["state"], observation_id, now)?;
003580     tx.execute("UPDATE bindings SET state_json=json_set(state_json,'$.native',json(?3),'$.observed_at_ms',?4,'$.native_sequence',?5,'$.native_observation_id',?6) WHERE binding_id=?1 AND generation=?2",params![id,generation,encoded,now,sequence,observation_id])?;
003581     if let Some(usage) = usage.as_ref() {
003582         super::native_usage::retain(&tx, &b, usage, observation_id)?;
003583         super::provider_conditions::observe_usage(
003584             &tx,
003585             &b,
003586             usage,
003587             sequence.ok_or_else(|| {
003588                 Error::new(
003589                     "USAGE_SEQUENCE_REQUIRED",

000175 fn write_private_new_with_barrier(
000176     path: &Path,
000177     data: &[u8],
000178     barrier: impl FnOnce(&Path) -> Result<()>,
000179 ) -> Result<()> {
000180     let target = private_target_path(path)?;
000181     if target_is_regular_file(&target)? {
000182         return Err(private_file_exists());
000183     }
000184     let temp = private_temp_path(&target)?;
000185     let result = (|| {
000186         let mut file = create_private_temp(&temp)?;
000187         file.write_all(data)?;
000188         file.sync_all()?;
000189         drop(file);
000190         #[cfg(feature = "test-support")]
000191         if let Some(result) = test_support::prepublication_fault(&target) {
000192             result?;
000193         }
000194         publish_new(&temp, &target)?;
000195         barrier(&target)
000196     })();
000197     if result.is_err() {
000198         let _ = fs::remove_file(&temp);
000199     }
000200     result
000201 }

000203 /// Publish exact bounded bytes, or converge on the same retained regular file.
000204 /// An existing file must pass its own file-sync and platform parent boundary;
000205 /// matching visible bytes alone never establish success. Other publication
000206 /// errors remain errors even if they left the target visible.
000207 pub fn write_private_exact(path: &Path, data: &[u8], maximum: u64) -> Result<()> {
000208     write_private_exact_with_barrier(path, data, maximum, durability_barrier)
000209 }
000210 
000211 fn write_private_exact_with_barrier(
000212     path: &Path,
000213     data: &[u8],
000214     maximum: u64,
000215     mut barrier: impl FnMut(&Path) -> Result<()>,
000216 ) -> Result<()> {
000217     if data.len() as u64 > maximum {
000218         return Err(Error::invalid("private file exceeds its size limit"));
000219     }
000220     match write_private_new_with_barrier(path, data, |target| {
000221         barrier(target).map_err(durability_unknown)
000222     }) {
000223         Ok(()) => Ok(()),
000224         Err(error) if error.code == "PRIVATE_FILE_ALREADY_EXISTS" => {
000225             read_private_durable_with_barrier(path, maximum, Some(data), barrier).map(|_| ())
000226         }
000227         Err(error) => Err(error),
000228     }
000229 }

000231 /// Read bounded regular bytes only after re-establishing their durability.
000232 /// Retain and recheck the opened identity and exact bytes across the boundary.
000233 /// Unix syncs the file and its parent; Windows flushes the retained file handle
000234 /// (opened for write access), with no claim of Unix directory-fsync equivalence.
000235 /// New Windows publication still uses the existing write-through move.
000236 pub fn read_private_durable(path: &Path, maximum: u64) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
000237     read_private_durable_with_barrier(path, maximum, None, durability_barrier)
000238 }

000240 fn read_private_durable_with_barrier(
000241     path: &Path,
000242     maximum: u64,
000243     expected: Option<&[u8]>,
000244     barrier: impl FnOnce(&Path) -> Result<()>,
000245 ) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
000246     let target = private_target_path(path)?;
000247     if !target_is_regular_file(&target)? {
000248         return Err(std::io::Error::from(std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound).into());
000249     }
000250     let mut options = OpenOptions::new();
000251     options.read(true);
000252     #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
000253     {
000254         use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
000255         options.custom_flags(libc::O_NOFOLLOW | libc::O_NONBLOCK);
000256     }
000257     #[cfg(windows)]
000258     {
000259         use std::os::windows::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
000260         use windows_sys::Win32::Storage::FileSystem::FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT;
000261         options
000262             .write(true)
000263             .custom_flags(FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT);
000264     }
000265     let mut file = options.open(&target)?;
000266     let changed = || {
000267         Error::new(
000268             "PRIVATE_FILE_CHANGED",
000269             "private file changed across durability boundary",
000270         )
000271     };
000272     if !opened_regular_file_matches_path(&file, &target)? {
000273         return Err(changed());
000274     }
000275     let bytes = read_bounded_file(&mut file, maximum)?;
000276     if expected.is_some_and(|expected| bytes != expected) {
000277         return Err(Error::conflict("retained private file bytes differ"));
000278     }
000279     file.sync_all()
000280         .map_err(|error| durability_unknown(error.into()))?;
000281     barrier(&target).map_err(durability_unknown)?;
000282     if !opened_regular_file_matches_path(&file, &target)? {
000283         return Err(changed());
000284     }
000285     file.rewind()?;
000286     if read_bounded_file(&mut file, maximum)? != bytes
000287         || !opened_regular_file_matches_path(&file, &target)?
000288     {
000289         return Err(changed());
000290     }
000291     Ok(bytes)
000292 }
000293 
000294 fn read_bounded_file(file: &mut fs::File, maximum: u64) -> Result<Vec<u8>> {
000295     let metadata = file.metadata()?;
000296     if !metadata.is_file() || metadata.len() > maximum {
000297         return Err(Error::invalid("private file must be bounded and regular"));
000298     }
000299     let mut bytes = Vec::new();
000300     file.take(maximum.saturating_add(1))
000301         .read_to_end(&mut bytes)?;
000302     if bytes.len() as u64 > maximum {
000303         return Err(Error::invalid("private file exceeds its size limit"));
000304     }
000305     Ok(bytes)
000306 }

000311         format!("private file durability boundary failed: {}", error.code),
000312     )
000313 }

000362         publish_replace(&temp, &target)?;
000363         barrier(&target)
000364     })();

000371 /// Remove an exact regular private file and sync its parent on Unix.
000372 /// Returns `false` when the file was already absent. On Windows the removal
000373 /// uses the platform file API; no parent-directory flush equivalence is

000385 /// Remove an exact regular private file and report the platform's durability
000386 /// boundary. Unix syncs the parent directory after unlinking or confirming
000387 /// that the path is already absent. Windows performs the file removal but
000388 /// reports `Unknown`: its documented write-through move does not establish an
000389 /// equivalent parent-directory barrier for `DeleteFile`.
000390 pub fn remove_private_acknowledged(path: &Path) -> Result<RemovalOutcome> {
000391     remove_private_acknowledged_with_barrier(path, sync_parent_directory)
000392 }
000393 
000394 fn remove_private_acknowledged_with_barrier(
000395     path: &Path,
000396     barrier: impl FnOnce(&Path) -> Result<()>,
000397 ) -> Result<RemovalOutcome> {
000398     let target = private_target_path(path)?;
000399     let existed = target_is_regular_file(&target)?;
000400     #[cfg(unix)]
000401     {
000402         if existed {
000403             fs::remove_file(&target)?;
000404         }
000405         barrier(&target).map_err(durability_unknown)?;
000406         Ok(RemovalOutcome {
000407             removed: existed,
000408             durability: RemovalDurability::Confirmed,
000409         })
000410     }
000411     #[cfg(windows)]
000412     {
000413         let _ = barrier;
000414         if existed {
000415             fs::remove_file(&target)?;
000416         }
000417         Ok(RemovalOutcome {
000418             removed: existed,
000419             durability: RemovalDurability::Unknown,
000420         })
000421     }
000422     #[cfg(not(any(unix, windows)))]
000423     {
000424         let _ = barrier;
000425         let _ = existed;
000426         Err(Error::new(
000427             "DURABILITY_UNSUPPORTED",
000428             "acknowledged removal requires a parent-directory durability barrier unavailable on this platform",
000429         ))
000430     }
000431 }

000433 /// Sync the parent directory on Unix. Windows currently has no directory
000434 /// flush implementation here, so this returns success without claiming that
000435 /// the directory entry itself is durable there.
000436 pub fn sync_parent_directory(path: &Path) -> Result<()> {
000437     let target = private_target_path(path)?;
000438     let parent = target
000439         .parent()
000440         .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("private file path has no parent directory"))?;
000441     #[cfg(unix)]
000442     {
000443         #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
000444         let directory = {
000445             use std::os::unix::fs::OpenOptionsExt;
000446             OpenOptions::new()
000447                 .read(true)
000448                 .custom_flags(libc::O_DIRECTORY | libc::O_NOFOLLOW)
000449                 .open(parent)?
000450         };
000451         #[cfg(not(target_os = "linux"))]
000452         let directory = fs::File::open(parent)?;
000453         directory.sync_all()?;
000454     }
000455     #[cfg(windows)]
000456     {
000457         let _ = parent;
000458     }
000459     #[cfg(not(any(unix, windows)))]
000460     {
000461         let _ = parent;
000462         return Err(Error::new(
000463             "DURABILITY_UNSUPPORTED",
000464             "parent-directory durability is unsupported on this platform",
000465         ));
000466     }
000467     Ok(())
000468 }

000470 fn private_target_path(path: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf> {
000471     if !path.is_absolute()
000472         && path
000473             .components()
000474             .any(|component| matches!(component, Component::Prefix(_)))
000475     {
000476         return Err(Error::invalid(
000477             "private file path has a drive-relative prefix",
000478         ));
000479     }
000480     let absolute = if path.is_absolute() {
000481         path.to_path_buf()
000482     } else {
000483         std::env::current_dir()?.join(path)
000484     };
000485     if absolute
000486         .components()
000487         .any(|component| matches!(component, Component::CurDir | Component::ParentDir))
000488         || absolute.file_name().is_none()
000489     {
000490         return Err(Error::invalid(
000491             "private file path must name one file without traversal components",
000492         ));
000493     }
000494     let parent = absolute
000495         .parent()
000496         .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("private file path has no parent directory"))?;
000497     let mut current = PathBuf::new();
000498     for component in parent.components() {
000499         current.push(component.as_os_str());
000500         if matches!(component, Component::Prefix(_)) {
000501             continue;
000502         }
000503         let metadata = fs::symlink_metadata(&current)?;
000504         if is_link_or_reparse(&metadata) || !metadata.is_dir() {
000505             return Err(Error::new(
000506                 "PRIVATE_PATH_INVALID",
000507                 "private file path cannot traverse links or non-directories",
000508             ));
000509         }
000510     }
000511     Ok(absolute)
000512 }
000513

000517             "PRIVATE_FILE_INVALID",
000518             "private file target is a link or is not a regular file",
000519         )),

000047 async fn run() -> Result<()> {
000048     let config_path = config_argument()?;
000049     let owner = CandidateManagedOwner::from_environment()?;
000050     let (config, credential) = AdapterConfig::read(&config_path)?;
000051     let boot_id = owner.boot_id().to_owned();
000052     let native_scope_key = native_scope_key()?;
000053 
000054     let mut host = HostSession::new(config, credential, owner, boot_id, native_scope_key);
000055     let initial_hello = host.connect_and_hello(None, false).await?;
000056     let mut controller = controller_from_hello(&host, &initial_hello)?;
000057     let spawner = OwnedNativeSpawner::new(initial_hello.managed_owner);
000058     let mut native: Option<NativeSession> = None;
000059     let mut native_started = false;
000060     let mut native_live = false;
000061     let mut dispatch_closed = false;
000062     // A new process has no native stream readback yet. Do not publish an empty
000063     // snapshot over the Store's last observation merely because hello returned
000064     // the retained root identity.
000065     let mut dirty = false;
000066 
000067     flush_reports(&mut host, &mut controller, &mut dirty, native_live).await?;
000068 
000069     loop {
000070         drain_ready_native(
000071             native.as_mut(),
000072             &mut controller,
000073             &mut dirty,
000074             &mut native_live,
000075             &mut dispatch_closed,
000076         )
000077         .await?;
000078         flush_reports(&mut host, &mut controller, &mut dirty, native_live).await?;
000079 
000080         let command = match host.next(&controller, native_live).await {
000081             Ok(command) => command,
000082             Err(error) if error.code == "MODULE_NEXT_UNCERTAIN" => {
000083                 // The Store may already have durably admitted a command, but
000084                 // no command identity reached this process. Do not request a
000085                 // second command or infer which Operation was returned.
000086                 let _ = host.reconnect(&controller, native_live).await;
000087                 let _ = flush_reports(&mut host, &mut controller, &mut dirty, native_live).await;
000088                 if let Some(native) = native.as_mut() {
000089                     native.shutdown_and_drain(&mut controller, true).await?;
000090                 }
000091                 return Err(error);
000092             }
000093             Err(error) => {
000094                 if let Some(native) = native.as_mut() {
000095                     native.shutdown_and_drain(&mut controller, true).await?;
000096                 }
000097                 return Err(error);
000098             }
000099         };
000100 
000101         let Some(command) = command else {
000102             continue;
000103         };
000104 
000105         if host.route.as_ref() != Some(&command.route) {
000106             controller.reject_command(&command, "MANAGER_ROUTE_CHANGED")?;
000107             dirty = true;
000108             flush_reports(&mut host, &mut controller, &mut dirty, native_live).await?;
000109             continue;
000110         }
000111 
000112         match command.method.as_str() {
000113             "agent.open" => {
000114                 if native_started || dispatch_closed || host.recovery_required {
000115                     controller.reject_command(
000116                         &command,
000117                         if host.recovery_required {
000118                             "RECOVERY_REQUIRED"
000119                         } else {
000120                             "NATIVE_SESSION_ALREADY_STARTED"
000121                         },
000122                     )?;
000123                     dirty = true;
000124                     flush_reports(&mut host, &mut controller, &mut dirty, native_live).await?;
000125                     continue;
000126                 }
000127                 if controller.begin_open(&command).is_err() {
000128                     controller.reject_command(&command, "NATIVE_SESSION_ALREADY_OPEN")?;
000129                     dirty = true;
000130                     flush_reports(&mut host, &mut controller, &mut dirty, native_live).await?;
000131                     continue;
000132                 }
000133 
000134                 let spec = match build_launch_spec(&host.config.native_executable, &command) {
000135                     Ok(spec) => spec,
000136                     Err(code) => {
000137                         controller.reject_open_before_spawn(code);
000138                         dirty = true;
000139                         flush_reports(&mut host, &mut controller, &mut dirty, native_live).await?;
000140                         continue;
000141                     }
000142                 };
000143                 let spawned = match spawner.spawn_owned(spec) {
000144                     Ok(child) => child,
000145                     Err(_) => {
000146                         controller.reject_open_before_spawn("NATIVE_SPAWN_FAILED");
000147                         dirty = true;
000148                         flush_reports(&mut host, &mut controller, &mut dirty, native_live).await?;
000149                         continue;
000150                     }
000151                 };
000152                 native_started = true;
000153                 let membership_verified = spawned.membership_error_code().is_none();
000154                 let mut session = NativeSession::start(spawned)?;
000155                 native_live = membership_verified;
000156 
000157                 if !membership_verified {
000158                     controller.finish_open_without_init();
000159                     dirty = true;
000160                     flush_reports(&mut host, &mut controller, &mut dirty, native_live).await?;
000161                     session.shutdown_and_drain(&mut controller, false).await?;
000162                     native_live = false;
000163                     dispatch_closed = true;
000164                     native = Some(session);
000165                     continue;
000166                 }
000167 
000168                 let open = wait_for_open(
000169                     &mut session,
000170                     &mut controller,
000171                     &mut host,
000172                     &mut dirty,
000173                     &command,
000174                 )
000175                 .await;

000774 fn is_transport_uncertain(error: &Error) -> bool {
000775     matches!(
000776         error.code.as_str(),
000777         "HOST_UNAVAILABLE" | "DISCONNECTED" | "OUTCOME_UNKNOWN" | "IO_ERROR"
000778     )

000780 
000781 fn retry_delay(attempt: usize) -> Duration {
000782     let multiplier = 1_u32.checked_shl(attempt.min(5) as u32).unwrap_or(32);
000783     Duration::from_millis(150_u64.saturating_mul(u64::from(multiplier)).min(4_800))
000784 }
000785 
000786 async fn flush_reports(
000787     host: &mut HostSession,
000788     controller: &mut Controller,
000789     dirty: &mut bool,
000790     native_live: bool,
000791 ) -> Result<()> {
000792     for value in controller.pending_receipts() {
000793         let operation_id = value
000794             .get("operation_id")
000795             .and_then(Value::as_str)
000796             .ok_or_else(|| {
000797                 Error::new("MODULE_OUTCOME_INVALID", "retained Operation ID is missing")
000798             })?
000799             .to_owned();
000800         let response = host
000801             .request_saved("module.outcome", value, controller, native_live)
000802             .await?;
000803         if response["recorded"] != true && response["superseded"] != true {
000804             return Err(Error::new(
000805                 "MODULE_OUTCOME_ACK_INVALID",
000806                 "manager did not acknowledge the exact saved Operation receipt",
000807             ));
000808         }
000809         controller.acknowledge_outcome(&operation_id);
000810         *dirty = true;
000811     }
000812 
000813     if *dirty {
000814         for _ in 0..MAX_OBSERVATION_RETRIES {
000815             let observation = controller.prepare_observation();
000816             let response = host
000817                 .request_saved(
000818                     "module.observe",
000819                     observation_params(&observation),
000820                     controller,
000821                     native_live,
000822                 )
000823                 .await?;
000824             if controller.acknowledge_observation(&response).is_some() {
000825                 controller.finish_observation();
000826                 *dirty = false;
000827                 return Ok(());
000828             }
000829             if response["stale"] == true {
000830                 *dirty = true;
000831                 continue;
000832             }
000833             if response["replayed"] == true && response["recorded"] == true {
000834                 *dirty = false;
000835                 return Ok(());
000836             }
000837             return Err(Error::new(
000838                 "MODULE_OBSERVATION_ACK_INVALID",
000839                 "manager did not acknowledge the saved observation",
000840             ));
000841         }
000842         return Err(Error::new(
000843             "MODULE_OBSERVATION_STALE",
000844             "manager did not accept a fresh ordered observation",
000845         ));
000846     }
000847     Ok(())
000848 }
000849 
000850 fn observation_params(
000851     observation: &swarm_antigravity_adapter::controller::PendingObservation,
000852 ) -> Value {
000853     json!({
000854         "event_id": observation.event_id,
000855         "sequence": observation.sequence,
000856         "state": observation.state,
000857     })
000858 }

000882 impl NativeSession {
000883     fn start(mut child: OwnedNativeChild) -> Result<Self> {

000885         let stdin = child
000886             .take_stdin()
000887             .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("NATIVE_PIPE_MISSING", "native stdin pipe is unavailable"))?;
000888         let stdout = child.take_stdout().ok_or_else(|| {
000889             Error::new("NATIVE_PIPE_MISSING", "native stdout pipe is unavailable")
000890         })?;
000891         let stderr = child.take_stderr().ok_or_else(|| {
000892             Error::new("NATIVE_PIPE_MISSING", "native stderr pipe is unavailable")
000893         })?;
000894 
000895         let (sender, events) = mpsc::channel(NATIVE_EVENT_QUEUE);
000896         let stdout_wait = tokio::spawn(async move {
000897             let mut reader = NativeLineReader::new(stdout);
000898             while let Some(line) = reader.next_line().await {
000899                 let event = match line {
000900                     Ok(line) => NativeEvent::Line(line),
000901                     Err(_) => NativeEvent::FrameError,
000902                 };
000903                 if sender.send(event).await.is_err() {
000904                     return;
000905                 }
000906             }
000907         });
000908         let stderr_wait = tokio::spawn(stderr::drain(stderr));
000909         let child_wait = tokio::spawn(async move {
000910             let mut child = child;
000911             child.wait().await
000912         });
000913         Ok(Self {
000914             stdin,
000915             events,

000663         // override is rejected here; readback is verified at agent.open.

000665             Error::new(
000666                 code,
000667                 "manager route carries an invalid native Antigravity model override",
000668             )
000669         })?;

000674             || self.generation.is_some_and(|old| old != generation)

000680             ));
000681         }
000682         self.binding_id = Some(binding_id.to_owned());
000683         self.generation = Some(generation);
000684         self.route = Some(route);
000685         Ok(())
000686     }

000688     async fn reconnect(&mut self, controller: &Controller, native_live: bool) -> Result<()> {
000689         self.link = None;
000690         self.connect_and_hello(Some(controller), native_live)
000691             .await
000692             .map(|_| ())
000693     }
000694 
000695     async fn request_saved(
000696         &mut self,
000697         method: &str,
000698         params: Value,
000699         controller: &Controller,
000700         native_live: bool,
000701     ) -> Result<Value> {
000702         for attempt in 0..MAX_HOST_ATTEMPTS {
000703             if self.link.is_none() {
000704                 self.reconnect(controller, native_live).await?;
000705             }
000706             let result = self
000707                 .link
000708                 .as_mut()
000709                 .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("HOST_UNAVAILABLE", "controller link is unavailable"))?
000710                 .request(method, params.clone())
000711                 .await;
000712             match result {
000713                 Ok(result) => return Ok(result),
000714                 Err(error) if is_transport_uncertain(&error) => {
000715                     self.link = None;
000716                     if attempt + 1 < MAX_HOST_ATTEMPTS {
000717                         time::sleep(retry_delay(attempt)).await;
000718                     }
000719                 }
000720                 Err(error) => return Err(error),
000721             }
000722         }
000723         Err(Error::new(
000724             "HOST_RETRY_EXHAUSTED",
000725             "controller did not acknowledge the saved idempotent request",
000726         ))
000727     }
000728 
000729     async fn next(
000730         &mut self,
000731         controller: &Controller,
000732         native_live: bool,
000733     ) -> Result<Option<RuntimeCommand>> {
000734         for attempt in 0..MAX_HOST_ATTEMPTS {
000735             if self.link.is_none() {
000736                 self.reconnect(controller, native_live).await?;
000737             }
000738             let result = self
000739                 .link
000740                 .as_mut()
000741                 .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("HOST_UNAVAILABLE", "controller link is unavailable"))?
000742                 .next(controller)
000743                 .await;
000744             match result {
000745                 Ok(command) => return Ok(command),
000746                 Err(error) if error.code == "OUTCOME_UNKNOWN" => {
000747                     self.link = None;
000748                     return Err(Error::new(
000749                         "MODULE_NEXT_UNCERTAIN",
000750                         "module.next may have durably admitted a command; do not request another",
000751                     ));
000752                 }
000753                 Err(error)
000754                     if matches!(error.code.as_str(), "HOST_UNAVAILABLE" | "DISCONNECTED") =>
000755                 {
000756                     self.link = None;
000757                     if attempt + 1 < MAX_HOST_ATTEMPTS {
000758                         time::sleep(retry_delay(attempt)).await;
000759                     }
000760                 }
000761                 Err(error) => {
000762                     self.link = None;
000763                     return Err(error);
000764                 }
000765             }
000766         }
000767         Err(Error::new(
000768             "HOST_RETRY_EXHAUSTED",
000769             "controller did not answer bounded module.next retries",
000770         ))
000771     }
000772 }
000773 
000774 fn is_transport_uncertain(error: &Error) -> bool {
000775     matches!(
000776         error.code.as_str(),
000777         "HOST_UNAVAILABLE" | "DISCONNECTED" | "OUTCOME_UNKNOWN" | "IO_ERROR"
000778     )
000779 }
000780 
000781 fn retry_delay(attempt: usize) -> Duration {
000782     let multiplier = 1_u32.checked_shl(attempt.min(5) as u32).unwrap_or(32);
000783     Duration::from_millis(150_u64.saturating_mul(u64::from(multiplier)).min(4_800))
000784 }

000439 fn controller_from_hello(host: &HostSession, hello: &VerifiedModuleHello) -> Result<Controller> {
000440     let binding_id = hello
000441         .response
000442         .get("binding_id")
000443         .and_then(Value::as_str)
000444         .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
000445         .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("MODULE_HELLO_ACK_INVALID", "binding identity is missing"))?;
000446     let generation = hello
000447         .response
000448         .get("generation")
000449         .and_then(Value::as_i64)
000450         .filter(|value| *value > 0)
000451         .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("MODULE_HELLO_ACK_INVALID", "binding generation is invalid"))?;
000452     let native_root_id = hello
000453         .response
000454         .get("native_root_id")
000455         .and_then(Value::as_str)
000456         .map(str::to_owned);
000457     let native_scope_key = hello
000458         .response
000459         .get("native_scope_key")
000460         .and_then(Value::as_str);
000461     if native_root_id.is_some() != native_scope_key.is_some()
000462         || native_scope_key.is_some_and(|scope| scope != host.native_scope_key)
000463     {
000464         return Err(Error::new(
000465             "NATIVE_IDENTITY_MISMATCH",
000466             "manager returned an incomplete or foreign native root identity",
000467         ));
000468     }
000469     let normalized_dispatch_enabled =
000470         swarm_antigravity_adapter::contract::normalized_dispatch_enabled(
000471             &swarm_antigravity_adapter::contract::claim()?,
000472         );
000473     Ok(Controller::new(
000474         host.boot_id.clone(),
000475         host.native_scope_key.clone(),
000476         binding_id.to_owned(),
000477         generation,
000478         native_root_id,
000479         normalized_dispatch_enabled,
000480     ))
000481 }

000483 fn config_argument() -> Result<PathBuf> {
000484     let mut args = env::args_os();
000485     let _program = args.next();
000486     let path = args
000487         .next()
000488         .map(PathBuf::from)
000489         .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("usage: swarm-antigravity <absolute-config.json>"))?;
000490     if args.next().is_some() || !path.is_absolute() {
000491         return Err(Error::invalid(
000492             "usage: swarm-antigravity <absolute-config.json>",
000493         ));
000494     }
000495     Ok(path)

000497 
000498 fn native_scope_key() -> Result<String> {
000499     let home = if cfg!(windows) {
000500         env::var_os("USERPROFILE").or_else(|| env::var_os("HOME"))
000501     } else {
000502         env::var_os("HOME")
000503     }
000504     .map(PathBuf::from)
000505     .filter(|path| path.is_absolute())
000506     .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("NATIVE_SCOPE_UNAVAILABLE", "home directory is unavailable"))?;
000507     let scope = home.join(".gemini").join("antigravity-cli");
000508     let scope = scope
000509         .to_str()
000510         .filter(|value| value.len() <= 32 * 1024 && !value.chars().any(char::is_control))
000511         .ok_or_else(|| Error::new("NATIVE_SCOPE_UNAVAILABLE", "native scope path is invalid"))?;
000512     Ok(format!("antigravity:{scope}"))
000513 }

000036 #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
000037 struct PendingPrompt {
000038     identity: OperationIdentity,
000039     conversation_id: String,
000040     result_ordinal_before: u64,
000041     stream_generation: u64,

000045 /// Immutable record of one completed native turn, keyed by the exact prompt
000046 /// Operation that settled it. Store ACKs and prunes the adapter's retry
000047 /// outbox, so a body lookup cannot depend on pending_receipts surviving. This
000048 /// mapping is a separate bounded same-process cache that keeps only what a
000049 /// later normalized result page needs: the exact receipt, conversation,
000050 /// ordinal, boot, and stream generation that prove which prompt owned which
000051 /// response. It is never sufficient to recover after this process exits.
000052 #[derive(Debug, Clone)]
000053 struct CompletedTurn {
000054     receipt: swarm_contracts::runtime::ModuleReceiptIdentity,
000055     conversation_id: String,
000056     result_ordinal: u64,
000057     boot_id: String,
000058     stream_generation: u64,
000059 }
000060 
000061 /// Stateful native turn reducer. The manager remains the sole durable
000062 /// operation/journal authority; its outbox and reconciliation summaries are
000063 /// bounded same-process caches and are never sufficient to recover after this
000064 /// process exits.
000065 pub struct Controller {
000066     boot_id: String,
000067     native_scope_key: String,
000068     binding_id: String,
000069     generation: i64,
000070     native_root_id: Option<String>,
000071     requested_model_id: Option<String>,
000072     model_readback_state: Option<&'static str>,
000073     open_failure_code: Option<&'static str>,
000074     normalized_dispatch_enabled: bool,
000075     pending_open: Option<OperationIdentity>,
000076     pending: Option<PendingPrompt>,
000077     stream: StreamState,
000078     completed_turns: BTreeMap<String, CompletedTurn>,
000079     pending_receipts: VecDeque<serde_json::Value>,
000080     journal: VecDeque<serde_json::Value>,
000081     observation_sequence: u64,
000082     pending_observation: Option<PendingObservation>,
000083 }

000156     pub fn may_request_next(&self) -> bool {
000157         // An agent.reconcile command may need one separate target receipt in
000158         // addition to its own outcome. Reserve both slots before module.next
000159         // can durably admit that command.
000160         self.pending_receipts.len().saturating_add(2) <= MAX_PENDING_RECEIPTS
000161     }

000163     pub fn begin_open(&mut self, command: &RuntimeCommand) -> Result<OperationIdentity> {
000164         let identity = self.identity_for(command)?;
000165         if identity.method != "agent.open" {
000166             return Err(swarm_contracts::error::Error::invalid(
000167                 "EXPECTED_AGENT_OPEN",
000168             ));
000169         }
000170         if self.pending_open.is_some() || self.native_root_id.is_some() {
000171             return Err(swarm_contracts::error::Error::new(
000172                 "SESSION_ALREADY_OPEN",
000173                 "this adapter process already owns an open native session",
000174             ));
000175         }
000176         let requested_model = selected_model_id(&command.route)
000177             .map_err(|code| {
000178                 swarm_contracts::error::Error::new(code, "native route model override is invalid")
000179             })?
000180             .map(str::to_owned);
000181         self.requested_model_id = requested_model;
000182         self.model_readback_state = None;
000183         self.open_failure_code = None;
000184         self.stream
000185             .set_resume_baseline_pending(command.input.get("resume_conversation_id").is_some());
000186         self.pending_open = Some(identity.clone());
000187         Ok(identity)
000188     }

000402     /// A failed or partial write is ambiguous. It produces `Unknown` and the
000403     /// caller must not try `write_all` again or resend the prompt on reconnect.
000404     pub async fn write_prompt<W>(&mut self, writer: &mut W, line: &[u8]) -> PromptWrite
000405     where
000406         W: AsyncWrite + Unpin,
000407     {
000408         if let Err(_error) = writer.write_all(line).await {
000409             return self
000410                 .pending_unknown("NATIVE_WRITE_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN")
000411                 .map(PromptWrite::Unknown)
000412                 .unwrap_or(PromptWrite::MissingPendingOperation);
000413         }
000414         if let Err(_error) = writer.flush().await {
000415             return self
000416                 .pending_unknown("NATIVE_WRITE_OUTCOME_UNKNOWN")
000417                 .map(PromptWrite::Unknown)
000418                 .unwrap_or(PromptWrite::MissingPendingOperation);
000419         }
000420         PromptWrite::Written
000421     }

000423     /// A result settles only the single in-flight prompt, for the same native
000424     /// conversation and an explicitly terminal status with response digest.
000425     pub fn settle_terminal(&mut self) -> Option<RuntimeOutcome> {
000426         let (identity, conversation_id, result_ordinal_before, dispatch_admission) = {
000427             let pending = self.pending.as_ref()?;
000428             (
000429                 pending.identity.clone(),
000430                 pending.conversation_id.clone(),
000431                 pending.result_ordinal_before,
000432                 pending.dispatch_admission.clone(),
000433             )
000434         };
000435         let (turn, disposition) = self
000436             .stream
000437             .terminal_for_after_foreign(&conversation_id, result_ordinal_before)?;
000438         let turn = turn.clone();
000439         // The stream predicate proves that this is the first own result after
000440         // the prompt baseline and that every earlier global ordinal was an
000441         // explicitly identified foreign conversation. Keep the generation
000442         // fence below: a retained result from another stream cannot settle it.
000443         let stream_generation = self.pending.as_ref()?.stream_generation;
000444         if stream_generation != self.stream.stream_generation {
000445             return None;
000446         }
000447         if turn.response_sha256.is_none() {
000448             return self.pending_unknown("NATIVE_RESULT_FINGERPRINT_UNAVAILABLE");
000449         }
000450         let outcome = terminal_outcome(
000451             &identity,
000452             &self.boot_id,
000453             &self.native_scope_key,
000454             &conversation_id,
000455             &turn,
000456             disposition,
000457             dispatch_admission.as_ref(),
000458         );
000459         self.pending = None;
000460         self.pending_observation = None;
000461         let _ = self.record_outcome(&outcome, &identity.method, &identity.module_receipt);
000462         self.remember_completed_turn(&identity, &conversation_id, &turn);
000463         Some(outcome)
000464     }

000469     }

000471     /// Read the exact response body this process retained for one sequential
000472     /// turn. The caller names the prompt's conversation, the exact next
000473     /// ordinal, and the stream generation that owned the write. This only
000474     /// reports what this boot actually observed; it never reconstructs a body
000475     /// from a digest and never attributes another turn's text to this prompt.
000476     /// A miss is reported as a closed diagnostic so the caller fails closed.
000477     pub fn retained_response_body(
000478         &self,
000479         conversation_id: &str,
000480         result_ordinal: u64,
000481         stream_generation: u64,
000482     ) -> std::result::Result<crate::stream::RetainedResponseBody, &'static str> {
000483         if self.native_root_id.as_deref() != Some(conversation_id) {
000484             return Err("NATIVE_RESULT_BODY_ORIGIN_MISMATCH");
000485         }
000486         if stream_generation != self.stream.stream_generation {
000487             return Err("NATIVE_RESULT_BODY_STALE_OWNER");
000488         }
000489         self.stream
000490             .retained_response_for(result_ordinal, conversation_id, stream_generation)
000491             .map_err(|miss| miss.diagnostic_code())
000492     }

000494     /// Read the response body this process retained for one exact sealed
000495     /// dispatch Operation. The completed-turn mapping is separate from the
000496     /// retry outbox, so a prior Store ACK that prunes pending_receipts cannot
000497     /// erase the causal lookup. Store's sealed origin names the Operation, and
000498     /// the caller supplies no ordinal: the sealed producer receipt, target
000499     /// digest, and this adapter's own recorded mapping must all agree before
000500     /// any body is read. Any disagreement, or a prompt this process never
000501     /// settled or settled under another boot, fails closed.
000502     pub fn retained_response_for_operation(
000503         &self,
000504         operation_id: &str,
000505         origin: &swarm_contracts::runtime::NormalizedResultOriginContext,
000506     ) -> std::result::Result<crate::stream::RetainedResponseBody, &'static str> {
000507         let completed = self
000508             .completed_turns
000509             .get(operation_id)
000510             .ok_or("NATIVE_RESULT_BODY_UNAVAILABLE")?;
000511         // The sealed origin must name this exact Operation, not merely an
000512         // Operation whose request hashes the same. Two different Operations can
000513         // carry an identical input digest, so digest equality alone would let a
000514         // prompt's body be published under another Operation's result. Require
000515         // the exact Operation id and the full typed receipt equality Store sealed.
000516         if operation_id != origin.target_operation_id
000517             || completed.receipt != origin.producer.module_receipt
000518         {
000519             return Err("NATIVE_RESULT_BODY_ORIGIN_MISMATCH");
000520         }
000521         if completed.boot_id != self.boot_id
000522             || completed.stream_generation != self.stream.stream_generation
000523         {
000524             return Err("NATIVE_RESULT_BODY_STALE_OWNER");
000525         }
000526         self.retained_response_body(
000527             &completed.conversation_id,
000528             completed.result_ordinal,
000529             completed.stream_generation,
000530         )
000531     }
000532

000550                 stream_generation: self.stream.stream_generation,
000551             },
000552         );
000553         while self.completed_turns.len() > crate::stream::MAX_RETAINED_RESPONSES {
000554             // Evict the lowest result ordinal, not the lexically first key.

000533     /// Record the immutable causal mapping for one settled turn. This runs
000534     /// after the outcome is recorded and never touches the retry outbox, so a
000535     /// later Store ACK that prunes pending_receipts still leaves this entry
000536     /// intact. It is bounded and evicted together with its retained body.
000537     fn remember_completed_turn(
000538         &mut self,
000539         identity: &OperationIdentity,
000540         conversation_id: &str,
000541         turn: &Turn,
000542     ) {
000543         self.completed_turns.insert(
000544             identity.operation_id.clone(),
000545             CompletedTurn {
000546                 receipt: identity.module_receipt.clone(),
000547                 conversation_id: conversation_id.to_owned(),
000548                 result_ordinal: turn.result_ordinal,
000549                 boot_id: self.boot_id.clone(),
000550                 stream_generation: self.stream.stream_generation,
000551             },
000552         );
000553         while self.completed_turns.len() > crate::stream::MAX_RETAINED_RESPONSES {
000554             // Evict the lowest result ordinal, not the lexically first key.
000555             // Operation ids are opaque UUIDs whose ordering carries no time
000556             // meaning, so a lexical eviction could discard the newest
000557             // completed prompt and leave an older one unreadable.
000558             let Some(oldest) = self
000559                 .completed_turns
000560                 .iter()
000561                 .min_by_key(|(_, turn)| turn.result_ordinal)
000562                 .map(|(operation_id, _)| operation_id.clone())
000563             else {
000564                 break;
000565             };
000566             self.completed_turns.remove(&oldest);
000567         }
000568     }

000573     pub fn read_command(&mut self, command: &RuntimeCommand) -> Result<RuntimeOutcome> {
000574         let identity = self.identity_for(command)?;

000584                     || self.stream.init.is_none()
000585                 {

000587                         &identity,
000588                         "NATIVE_HISTORY_UNAVAILABLE",
000589                         "Antigravity exposes no exact native history readback for this root",
000590                     ));
000591                 }

000593                     "completion_condition": "current_process_native_observation",
000594                     "target": target,
000595                     "snapshot": self.stream.snapshot(),

000600                     .input

000630                             "completion_condition": "native_history_unavailable",
000631                             "target_operation_id": target,
000632                             "reconcile_operation_id": identity.operation_id,
000633                         }),
000634                     };

000636                         &target_outcome,

000645                     native_root_id: self.native_root_id.clone(),

000647                     native_input_id: None,

000649                         "diagnostic_code": "NATIVE_HISTORY_UNAVAILABLE",
000650                         "completion_condition": "native_history_unavailable",

000840     fn pending_unknown(&mut self, diagnostic_code: &'static str) -> Option<RuntimeOutcome> {
000841         let pending = self.pending.take()?;
000842         let outcome = RuntimeOutcome {
000843             operation_id: pending.identity.operation_id.clone(),
000844             outcome: EffectOutcome::Unknown,
000845             native_scope_key: Some(self.native_scope_key.clone()),
000846             native_root_id: Some(pending.conversation_id),
000847             turn_id: None,
000848             native_input_id: None,
000849             details: json!({ "diagnostic_code": diagnostic_code }),
000850         };
000851         let _ = self.record_outcome(
000852             &outcome,
000853             &pending.identity.method,
000854             &pending.identity.module_receipt,
000855         );
000856         Some(outcome)
000857     }
000858 
000859     fn record_outcome(
000860         &mut self,
000861         outcome: &RuntimeOutcome,
000862         method: &str,
000863         receipt: &swarm_contracts::runtime::ModuleReceiptIdentity,
000864     ) -> Result<()> {
000865         let value = module_receipt::serialize_outcome(outcome, receipt).map_err(|_| {
000866             swarm_contracts::error::Error::new(
000867                 "MODULE_OUTCOME_INVALID",
000868                 "adapter could not retain its bounded operation receipt",
000869             )
000870         })?;
000871         self.pending_receipts
000872             .retain(|entry| entry["operation_id"].as_str() != Some(outcome.operation_id.as_str()));
000873         if self.pending_receipts.len() == MAX_PENDING_RECEIPTS {
000874             return Err(swarm_contracts::error::Error::new(
000875                 "MODULE_OUTCOME_OUTBOX_FULL",
000876                 "acknowledge pending manager receipts before admitting another operation",
000877             ));
000878         }
000879         self.pending_receipts.push_back(value);
000880         let summary = json!({
000881             "operation_id": outcome.operation_id,
000882             "method": method,
000883             "outcome": match outcome.outcome {
000884                 EffectOutcome::Accepted => "accepted",
000885                 EffectOutcome::Applied => "applied",
000886                 EffectOutcome::Rejected => "rejected",
000887                 EffectOutcome::Unknown => "unknown",
000888             },
000889             "completion_condition": outcome.details.get("completion_condition"),
000890             "diagnostic_code": outcome.details.get("diagnostic_code"),
000891         });
000892         self.journal
000893             .retain(|entry| entry["operation_id"].as_str() != Some(outcome.operation_id.as_str()));
000894         if self.journal.len() == MAX_RECONCILE_RECEIPTS {
000895             self.journal.pop_front();
000896         }
000897         self.journal.push_back(summary);
000898         Ok(())
000899     }
000900 
000901     fn native_scope_key_if_root_known(&self) -> Option<String> {
000902         self.native_root_id
000903             .as_ref()
000904             .map(|_| self.native_scope_key.clone())
000905     }
000906 }

000908 fn terminal_outcome(
000909     identity: &OperationIdentity,
000910     boot_id: &str,
000911     scope_key: &str,
000912     conversation_id: &str,
000913     turn: &Turn,
000914     disposition: TerminalDisposition,
000915     dispatch_admission: Option<&TaskDispatchAdmissionReceipt>,
000916 ) -> RuntimeOutcome {
000917     let status = turn.status.as_str();
000918     let reference = json!({
000919         "input_operation_id": identity.operation_id,
000920         "native_conversation_id": conversation_id,
000921         "bridge_boot_id": boot_id,
000922         "result_ordinal": turn.result_ordinal,
000923         "response_sha256": turn.response_sha256,
000924         "status": status,
000925     });
000926     let mut details = json!({
000927         "completion_condition": "native_terminal_result_observed",
000928         "turn_status": status,
000929         "num_turns": turn.num_turns,
000930         "duration_seconds": turn.duration_seconds,
000931         "duration_delta_seconds": turn.duration_delta_seconds,
000932         "usage": turn.usage,
000933         "local_execution_ref": reference,
000934     });
000935     if matches!(disposition, TerminalDisposition::Applied)
000936         && let Some(admission) = dispatch_admission
000937     {
000938         // The stream proves that this process observed a terminal result, but
000939         // exposes no native input-parent edge. Retain dispatch admission without
000940         // promoting the local ordinal into execution or Task completion proof.
000941         details["execution_complete"] = json!(false);
000942         details["task_completion"] = json!("unknown");
000943         details["dispatch_admission"] =
000944             serde_json::to_value(admission).unwrap_or(serde_json::Value::Null);
000945         details["prompt_contract_revision"] = json!(TASK_PROMPT_CONTRACT_REVISION);
000946     }
000947     RuntimeOutcome {
000948         operation_id: identity.operation_id.clone(),
000949         outcome: match disposition {
000950             TerminalDisposition::Applied => EffectOutcome::Applied,
000951             TerminalDisposition::Rejected => EffectOutcome::Rejected,
000952         },
000953         native_scope_key: Some(scope_key.to_owned()),
000954         native_root_id: Some(conversation_id.to_owned()),
000955         // The CLI result carries no native turn identifier. Ordinal is only a
000956         // local sequential association, so keep the native turn field empty.
000957         turn_id: None,
000958         native_input_id: None,
000959         details,
000960     }
000961 }

000799             .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_i64)
000800             .filter(|id| *id > 0);
000801         if !recorded || stale || observation_id.is_none() {
000802             if stale
000803                 || response
000804                     .get("replayed")
000805                     .and_then(serde_json::Value::as_bool)
000806                     == Some(true)
000807             {
000808                 self.retry_observation_after_stale();
000809             }
000810             return None;
000811         }
000812         if let (Some(pending), Some(observation_id)) =
000813             (self.pending_observation.as_mut(), observation_id)
000814         {
000815             pending.observation_id = Some(observation_id);
000816             Some(observation_id)
000817         } else {
000818             None
000819         }
000820     }

000836     pub fn snapshot(&self) -> serde_json::Value {
