000840     fn pending_unknown(&mut self, diagnostic_code: &'static str) -> Option<RuntimeOutcome> {
000841         let pending = self.pending.take()?;
000842         let outcome = RuntimeOutcome {
000843             operation_id: pending.identity.operation_id.clone(),
000844             outcome: EffectOutcome::Unknown,
000845             native_scope_key: Some(self.native_scope_key.clone()),
000846             native_root_id: Some(pending.conversation_id),
000847             turn_id: None,
000848             native_input_id: None,
000849             details: json!({ "diagnostic_code": diagnostic_code }),
000850         };
000851         let _ = self.record_outcome(
000852             &outcome,
000853             &pending.identity.method,
000854             &pending.identity.module_receipt,
000855         );
000856         Some(outcome)
000857     }
000858 
000859     fn record_outcome(
000860         &mut self,
000861         outcome: &RuntimeOutcome,
000862         method: &str,
000863         receipt: &swarm_contracts::runtime::ModuleReceiptIdentity,
000864     ) -> Result<()> {
000865         let value = module_receipt::serialize_outcome(outcome, receipt).map_err(|_| {
000866             swarm_contracts::error::Error::new(
000867                 "MODULE_OUTCOME_INVALID",
000868                 "adapter could not retain its bounded operation receipt",
000869             )
000870         })?;
000871         self.pending_receipts
000872             .retain(|entry| entry["operation_id"].as_str() != Some(outcome.operation_id.as_str()));
000873         if self.pending_receipts.len() == MAX_PENDING_RECEIPTS {
000874             return Err(swarm_contracts::error::Error::new(
000875                 "MODULE_OUTCOME_OUTBOX_FULL",
000876                 "acknowledge pending manager receipts before admitting another operation",
000877             ));
000878         }
000879         self.pending_receipts.push_back(value);
000880         let summary = json!({
000881             "operation_id": outcome.operation_id,
000882             "method": method,
000883             "outcome": match outcome.outcome {
000884                 EffectOutcome::Accepted => "accepted",
000885                 EffectOutcome::Applied => "applied",
000886                 EffectOutcome::Rejected => "rejected",
000887                 EffectOutcome::Unknown => "unknown",
000888             },
000889             "completion_condition": outcome.details.get("completion_condition"),
000890             "diagnostic_code": outcome.details.get("diagnostic_code"),
000891         });
000892         self.journal
000893             .retain(|entry| entry["operation_id"].as_str() != Some(outcome.operation_id.as_str()));
000894         if self.journal.len() == MAX_RECONCILE_RECEIPTS {
000895             self.journal.pop_front();
000896         }
000897         self.journal.push_back(summary);
000898         Ok(())
000899     }
000900 
000901     fn native_scope_key_if_root_known(&self) -> Option<String> {
000902         self.native_root_id
000903             .as_ref()
000904             .map(|_| self.native_scope_key.clone())
000905     }
000906 }

000908 fn terminal_outcome(
000909     identity: &OperationIdentity,
000910     boot_id: &str,
000911     scope_key: &str,
000912     conversation_id: &str,
000913     turn: &Turn,
000914     disposition: TerminalDisposition,
000915     dispatch_admission: Option<&TaskDispatchAdmissionReceipt>,
000916 ) -> RuntimeOutcome {
000917     let status = turn.status.as_str();
000918     let reference = json!({
000919         "input_operation_id": identity.operation_id,
000920         "native_conversation_id": conversation_id,
000921         "bridge_boot_id": boot_id,
000922         "result_ordinal": turn.result_ordinal,
000923         "response_sha256": turn.response_sha256,
000924         "status": status,
000925     });
000926     let mut details = json!({
000927         "completion_condition": "native_terminal_result_observed",
000928         "turn_status": status,
000929         "num_turns": turn.num_turns,
000930         "duration_seconds": turn.duration_seconds,
000931         "duration_delta_seconds": turn.duration_delta_seconds,
000932         "usage": turn.usage,
000933         "local_execution_ref": reference,
000934     });
000935     if matches!(disposition, TerminalDisposition::Applied)
000936         && let Some(admission) = dispatch_admission
000937     {
000938         // The stream proves that this process observed a terminal result, but
000939         // exposes no native input-parent edge. Retain dispatch admission without
000940         // promoting the local ordinal into execution or Task completion proof.
000941         details["execution_complete"] = json!(false);
000942         details["task_completion"] = json!("unknown");
000943         details["dispatch_admission"] =
000944             serde_json::to_value(admission).unwrap_or(serde_json::Value::Null);
000945         details["prompt_contract_revision"] = json!(TASK_PROMPT_CONTRACT_REVISION);
000946     }
000947     RuntimeOutcome {
000948         operation_id: identity.operation_id.clone(),
000949         outcome: match disposition {
000950             TerminalDisposition::Applied => EffectOutcome::Applied,
000951             TerminalDisposition::Rejected => EffectOutcome::Rejected,
000952         },
000953         native_scope_key: Some(scope_key.to_owned()),
000954         native_root_id: Some(conversation_id.to_owned()),
000955         // The CLI result carries no native turn identifier. Ordinal is only a
000956         // local sequential association, so keep the native turn field empty.
000957         turn_id: None,
000958         native_input_id: None,
000959         details,
000960     }

000471     /// Read the exact response body this process retained for one sequential
000472     /// turn. The caller names the prompt's conversation, the exact next
000473     /// ordinal, and the stream generation that owned the write. This only
000474     /// reports what this boot actually observed; it never reconstructs a body
000475     /// from a digest and never attributes another turn's text to this prompt.
000476     /// A miss is reported as a closed diagnostic so the caller fails closed.
000477     pub fn retained_response_body(
000478         &self,
000479         conversation_id: &str,
000480         result_ordinal: u64,
000481         stream_generation: u64,
000482     ) -> std::result::Result<crate::stream::RetainedResponseBody, &'static str> {
000483         if self.native_root_id.as_deref() != Some(conversation_id) {
000484             return Err("NATIVE_RESULT_BODY_ORIGIN_MISMATCH");
000485         }
000486         if stream_generation != self.stream.stream_generation {
000487             return Err("NATIVE_RESULT_BODY_STALE_OWNER");
000488         }
000489         self.stream
000490             .retained_response_for(result_ordinal, conversation_id, stream_generation)
000491             .map_err(|miss| miss.diagnostic_code())
000492     }
000493 
000494     /// Read the response body this process retained for one exact sealed
000495     /// dispatch Operation. The completed-turn mapping is separate from the
000496     /// retry outbox, so a prior Store ACK that prunes pending_receipts cannot
000497     /// erase the causal lookup. Store's sealed origin names the Operation, and
000498     /// the caller supplies no ordinal: the sealed producer receipt, target
000499     /// digest, and this adapter's own recorded mapping must all agree before
000500     /// any body is read. Any disagreement, or a prompt this process never
000501     /// settled or settled under another boot, fails closed.
000502     pub fn retained_response_for_operation(
000503         &self,
000504         operation_id: &str,
000505         origin: &swarm_contracts::runtime::NormalizedResultOriginContext,
000506     ) -> std::result::Result<crate::stream::RetainedResponseBody, &'static str> {
000507         let completed = self
000508             .completed_turns
000509             .get(operation_id)
000510             .ok_or("NATIVE_RESULT_BODY_UNAVAILABLE")?;
000511         // The sealed origin must name this exact Operation, not merely an
000512         // Operation whose request hashes the same. Two different Operations can
000513         // carry an identical input digest, so digest equality alone would let a
000514         // prompt's body be published under another Operation's result. Require
000515         // the exact Operation id and the full typed receipt equality Store sealed.
000516         if operation_id != origin.target_operation_id
000517             || completed.receipt != origin.producer.module_receipt
000518         {
000519             return Err("NATIVE_RESULT_BODY_ORIGIN_MISMATCH");
000520         }
000521         if completed.boot_id != self.boot_id
000522             || completed.stream_generation != self.stream.stream_generation
000523         {
000524             return Err("NATIVE_RESULT_BODY_STALE_OWNER");
000525         }
000526         self.retained_response_body(
000527             &completed.conversation_id,
000528             completed.result_ordinal,
000529             completed.stream_generation,
000530         )
000531     }
000532 
000533     /// Record the immutable causal mapping for one settled turn. This runs
000534     /// after the outcome is recorded and never touches the retry outbox, so a
000535     /// later Store ACK that prunes pending_receipts still leaves this entry
000536     /// intact. It is bounded and evicted together with its retained body.
000537     fn remember_completed_turn(
000538         &mut self,
000539         identity: &OperationIdentity,
000540         conversation_id: &str,
000541         turn: &Turn,
000542     ) {
000543         self.completed_turns.insert(
000544             identity.operation_id.clone(),
000545             CompletedTurn {
000546                 receipt: identity.module_receipt.clone(),
000547                 conversation_id: conversation_id.to_owned(),
000548                 result_ordinal: turn.result_ordinal,
000549                 boot_id: self.boot_id.clone(),
000550                 stream_generation: self.stream.stream_generation,
000551             },
000552         );
000553         while self.completed_turns.len() > crate::stream::MAX_RETAINED_RESPONSES {
000554             // Evict the lowest result ordinal, not the lexically first key.
000555             // Operation ids are opaque UUIDs whose ordering carries no time
000556             // meaning, so a lexical eviction could discard the newest
000557             // completed prompt and leave an older one unreadable.
000558             let Some(oldest) = self
000559                 .completed_turns
000560                 .iter()
000561                 .min_by_key(|(_, turn)| turn.result_ordinal)
000562                 .map(|(operation_id, _)| operation_id.clone())
000563             else {
000564                 break;
000565             };
000566             self.completed_turns.remove(&oldest);
000567         }
000568     }

000815     fn apply_result(&mut self, event: &Value) {
000816         let Some(payload) = event.get("result").and_then(Value::as_object) else {
000817             self.gaps = self.gaps.saturating_add(1);
000818             return;
000819         };
000820         let Some(status) = payload.get("status").and_then(Value::as_str) else {
000821             self.gaps = self.gaps.saturating_add(1);
000822             return;
000823         };
000824         self.result_ordinal = self.result_ordinal.saturating_add(1);
000825         let status = bounded_string(status, 64);
000826         let response = payload.get("response").and_then(Value::as_str);
000827         let conversation_id = payload
000828             .get("conversation_id")
000829             .and_then(Value::as_str)
000830             .or_else(|| event.get("conversation_id").and_then(Value::as_str))
000831             .map(|value| bounded_string(value, 512));
000832         let num_turns = payload.get("num_turns").and_then(Value::as_u64);
000833         let duration_seconds = payload
000834             .get("duration_seconds")
000835             .and_then(nonnegative_finite_f64);
000836         let cumulative_usage = payload.get("usage").and_then(UsageSnapshot::from_value);
000837         let (usage, duration_delta_seconds) = self.usage_evidence(
000838             &status,
000839             conversation_id.as_deref(),
000840             self.result_ordinal,
000841             num_turns,
000842             duration_seconds,
000843             cumulative_usage,
000844         );
000845         let turn = Turn {
000846             status,
000847             conversation_id,
000848             result_ordinal: self.result_ordinal,
000849             response_sha256: response.map(sha256_hex),
000850             response_chars: response
000851                 .map(|value| value.chars().count() as u64)
000852                 .unwrap_or(0),
000853             num_turns,
000854             duration_seconds,
000855             duration_delta_seconds,
000856             usage,
000857         };
000858         if self.turns.len() == MAX_TURNS {
000859             self.turns.pop_front();
000860             self.gaps = self.gaps.saturating_add(1);
000861         }
000862         self.turns.push_back(turn.clone());
000863         if let Some(response) = response
000864             && let Some(response_sha256) = turn.response_sha256.as_deref()
000865         {
000866             if let Some(conversation_id) = turn.conversation_id.as_deref() {
000867                 self.retain_response(
000868                     self.result_ordinal,
000869                     conversation_id,
000870                     &turn.status,
000871                     response,
000872                     response_sha256,
000873                 );
000874             } else {
000875                 // A response with no conversation identity cannot be attributed
000876                 // to any root. Drop it instead of retaining an unownable body.
000877                 self.gaps = self.gaps.saturating_add(1);
000878             }
000879         }
000880         if self.phase == Phase::AwaitingInit {
000881             if matches!(turn.status.as_str(), "ERROR" | "CANCELED" | "INTERRUPTED") {
000882                 self.phase = Phase::InitFailed;
000883                 self.init_failure_status = Some(turn.status);
000884                 self.execution = "init_failed";
000885             } else {
000886                 // A pre-init SUCCESS/WAITING/RUNNING/unknown result does not
000887                 // prove that initialization failed or establish a session.
000888                 self.gaps = self.gaps.saturating_add(1);
000889             }
000890             self.resumed_baseline_pending = false;
000891             self.last_terminal_usage = None;
000892             return;
000893         }
000894         self.execution = match turn.status.as_str() {
000895             "SUCCESS" => "turn_completed",
000896             "WAITING" => "turn_waiting",
000897             "RUNNING" => "turn_open",
000898             _ => "turn_failed",
000899         };
000900         self.remember_terminal_usage(&turn);
000901     }
000902 }

000465     /// Read the retained response body for one exact target, or fail closed.
000466     /// Every field the caller expects must match: the exact next ordinal, the
000467     /// same conversation, and the same owned boot and stream generation. A
000468     /// missing, mismatched, or stale entry is reported as a typed cause rather
000469     /// than substituted with any other retained body.
000470     pub fn retained_response_for(
000471         &self,
000472         result_ordinal: u64,
000473         expected_conversation: &str,
000474         expected_stream_generation: u64,
000475     ) -> Result<RetainedResponseBody, RetainedResponseMiss> {
000476         let Some(retained) = self.retained_responses.get(&result_ordinal) else {
000477             return Err(RetainedResponseMiss::Absent);
000478         };
000479         if retained.conversation_id != expected_conversation {
000480             return Err(RetainedResponseMiss::ConversationMismatch);
000481         }
000482         if retained.stream_generation != expected_stream_generation
000483             || retained.stream_generation != self.stream_generation
000484         {
000485             return Err(RetainedResponseMiss::StaleOwner);
000486         }
000487         Ok(RetainedResponseBody {
000488             status: retained.status.clone(),
000489             response_sha256: retained.response_sha256.clone(),
000490             body: retained.body.clone(),
000491         })
000492     }

000510             "exit_code": self.exit_code,

000514     fn apply_init(&mut self, event: &Value) {
000515         if self.init.is_some() {
000516             self.gaps = self.gaps.saturating_add(1);
000517             return;
000518         }
000519         let Some(conversation_id) = event
000520             .get("conversation_id")
000521             .and_then(Value::as_str)
000522             .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
000523         else {
000524             self.gaps = self.gaps.saturating_add(1);
000525             return;
000526         };
000527         let payload = event.get("init").unwrap_or(&Value::Null);
000528         let (tools, tools_truncated) =
000529             bounded_string_array(payload.get("tools"), MAX_INIT_TOOLS, 256);
000530         if tools_truncated {
000531             self.gaps = self.gaps.saturating_add(1);
000532         }
000533         self.init = Some(Init {
000534             conversation_id: bounded_string(conversation_id, 512),
000535             cwd: string_field(payload, "cwd", 2048),
000536             tools,
000537             permission_mode: string_field(payload, "permission_mode", 128),
000538             model: string_field(payload, "model", 256),
000539             agent: string_field(payload, "agent", 256),
000540         });
000541         if self.phase == Phase::AwaitingInit {
000542             self.phase = Phase::Ready;
000543         }
000544         if self.execution == "not_started" {
000545             self.execution = "running";
000546         }
000547     }

000549     fn apply_step(&mut self, event: &Value) {
000550         let Some(payload) = event.get("step_update").and_then(Value::as_object) else {
000551             self.gaps = self.gaps.saturating_add(1);
000552             return;
000553         };
000554         let Some(index) = payload.get("step_index").and_then(Value::as_u64) else {
000555             self.gaps = self.gaps.saturating_add(1);
000556             return;
000557         };
000558         if let (Some(root), Some(carrier)) = (
000559             self.init.as_ref().map(|init| init.conversation_id.as_str()),
000560             payload.get("conversation_id").and_then(Value::as_str),
000561         ) && carrier != root
000562         {
000563             self.gaps = self.gaps.saturating_add(1);
000564             return;
000565         }
000566 
000567         if !self.steps.contains_key(&index) && self.steps.len() >= MAX_STEPS {
000568             if let Some(oldest) = self.steps.keys().next().copied() {
000569                 self.steps.remove(&oldest);
000570             }
000571             self.gaps = self.gaps.saturating_add(1);
000572         }
000573         let step = self.steps.entry(index).or_insert_with(|| Step {
000574             step_index: index,
000575             state: None,
000576             step_type: None,
000577             tool_name: None,
000578             text_chars: 0,
000579             usage: None,
000580             tool_error_type: None,
000581             subagent_ids: Vec::new(),
000582         });
000583         step.state = string_field_from_map(payload, "state", 64).or(step.state.take());
000584         step.step_type = string_field_from_map(payload, "step_type", 128).or(step.step_type.take());
000585         step.tool_name = string_field_from_map(payload, "tool_name", 256).or(step.tool_name.take());
000586         if let Some(delta) = payload.get("text_delta").and_then(Value::as_str) {
000587             step.text_chars = step
000588                 .text_chars
000589                 .saturating_add(delta.chars().count() as u64)
000590                 .min(MAX_TEXT_CHARS_PER_STEP);
000591         }
000592         if let Some(raw_usage) = payload.get("usage") {
000593             if let Some(usage) = UsageSnapshot::from_value(raw_usage) {
000594                 step.usage = Some(usage);
000595             } else {
000596                 self.gaps = self.gaps.saturating_add(1);
000597             }
000598         }

000345         // Departure does not invalidate bodies observed and sealed before it.
000346         // Exact Operation and generation checks still fence every later page.
000347         self.phase = Phase::StreamEnded;
000348         self.execution = "stream_ended";
000349         self.exit_code = exit_code;
000350     }

000352     pub fn latest_turn(&self) -> Option<&Turn> {
000353         self.turns.back()
000354     }
000355 
000356     pub fn terminal_for(
000357         &self,
000358         expected_conversation: &str,
000359     ) -> Option<(&Turn, TerminalDisposition)> {
000360         let turn = self.latest_turn()?;
000361         if turn.conversation_id.as_deref() != Some(expected_conversation) {
000362             return None;
000363         }
000364         let disposition = match turn.status.as_str() {
000365             "SUCCESS" => TerminalDisposition::Applied,
000366             "ERROR" | "CANCELED" | "INTERRUPTED" => TerminalDisposition::Rejected,
000367             _ => return None,
000368         };
000369         Some((turn, disposition))
000370     }
000371 
000372     /// Return the unique own terminal after a prompt baseline only when the
000373     /// complete retained global ordinal range through the current result is
000374     /// contiguous. Every other result must explicitly identify a foreign
000375     /// conversation; multiple own results, an own nonterminal, an unknown
000376     /// conversation, a missing ordinal, or overflow is ambiguous.
000377     pub fn terminal_for_after_foreign(
000378         &self,
000379         expected_conversation: &str,
000380         result_ordinal_before: u64,
000381     ) -> Option<(&Turn, TerminalDisposition)> {
000382         if expected_conversation.is_empty() {
000383             return None;
000384         }
000385 
000386         let first_ordinal = result_ordinal_before.checked_add(1)?;
000387         let ordinal_after_current = self.result_ordinal.checked_add(1)?;
000388         let mut expected_ordinal = first_ordinal;
000389         let mut own_terminal = None;
000390         for turn in &self.turns {
000391             if turn.result_ordinal < first_ordinal {
000392                 continue;
000393             }
000394             if turn.result_ordinal >= ordinal_after_current
000395                 || turn.result_ordinal != expected_ordinal
000396             {
000397                 return None;
000398             }
000399 
000400             let conversation_id = turn
000401                 .conversation_id
000402                 .as_deref()
000403                 .filter(|conversation_id| !conversation_id.is_empty())?;
000404             if conversation_id == expected_conversation {
000405                 if own_terminal.is_some() {
000406                     return None;
000407                 }
000408                 let disposition = match turn.status.as_str() {
000409                     "SUCCESS" => TerminalDisposition::Applied,
000410                     "ERROR" | "CANCELED" | "INTERRUPTED" => TerminalDisposition::Rejected,
000411                     _ => return None,
000412                 };
000413                 own_terminal = Some((turn, disposition));
000414             }
000415 
000416             // Only a result carrying an explicit, non-empty different
000417             // conversation ID can be certified as foreign.
000418             expected_ordinal = expected_ordinal.checked_add(1)?;
000419         }
000420         if expected_ordinal != ordinal_after_current {
000421             return None;
000422         }
000423         own_terminal
000424     }
000425 
000426     /// Retain one observed response body under its exact result ordinal. Only
000427     /// the body carried by that ordinal's own result event is accepted, and
000428     /// only when its recorded digest matches, so a retained body can never be
000429     /// substituted for another turn's answer.
000430     fn retain_response(
000431         &mut self,
000432         result_ordinal: u64,
000433         conversation_id: &str,
000434         status: &str,
000435         response: &str,
000436         response_sha256: &str,
000437     ) {
000438         let body = bounded_string(response, MAX_RESPONSE_CHARS);
000439         if sha256_hex(&body) != response_sha256 || body.chars().count() < response.chars().count() {
000440             // A body that had to be truncated cannot be represented by the
000441             // digest the native event declared. Drop it and fail closed rather
000442             // than publish a page whose payload digest is a lie.
000443             self.retained_responses.remove(&result_ordinal);
000444             self.gaps = self.gaps.saturating_add(1);
000445             return;
000446         }
000447         self.retained_responses.insert(
000448             result_ordinal,
000449             RetainedResponse {
000450                 conversation_id: conversation_id.to_owned(),
000451                 stream_generation: self.stream_generation,
000452                 status: status.to_owned(),
000453                 response_sha256: response_sha256.to_owned(),
000454                 body,
000455             },
000456         );
000457         while self.retained_responses.len() > MAX_RETAINED_RESPONSES {
000458             let Some(oldest) = self.retained_responses.keys().next().copied() else {
000459                 break;
000460             };
000461             self.retained_responses.remove(&oldest);
000462         }
000463     }
000464 
000465     /// Read the retained response body for one exact target, or fail closed.
000466     /// Every field the caller expects must match: the exact next ordinal, the
000467     /// same conversation, and the same owned boot and stream generation. A
000468     /// missing, mismatched, or stale entry is reported as a typed cause rather
000469     /// than substituted with any other retained body.
000470     pub fn retained_response_for(
000471         &self,
000472         result_ordinal: u64,
000473         expected_conversation: &str,
000474         expected_stream_generation: u64,
000475     ) -> Result<RetainedResponseBody, RetainedResponseMiss> {
000476         let Some(retained) = self.retained_responses.get(&result_ordinal) else {
000477             return Err(RetainedResponseMiss::Absent);
000478         };
000479         if retained.conversation_id != expected_conversation {
000480             return Err(RetainedResponseMiss::ConversationMismatch);
000481         }
000482         if retained.stream_generation != expected_stream_generation
000483             || retained.stream_generation != self.stream_generation
000484         {
000485             return Err(RetainedResponseMiss::StaleOwner);
000486         }
000487         Ok(RetainedResponseBody {
000488             status: retained.status.clone(),
000489             response_sha256: retained.response_sha256.clone(),
000490             body: retained.body.clone(),
000491         })
000492     }
000493 
000494     pub fn snapshot(&self) -> Value {

000497             "init": self.init,
000498             "init_failure_status": self.init_failure_status,
000499             "execution": self.execution,

000501             "children": self.children.values().collect::<Vec<_>>(),
000502             "turns": self.turns,
000503             "result_ordinal": self.result_ordinal,
000504             "tool_errors": self.tool_errors,
000505             "native_events_seen": self.native_events_seen,
000506             "malformed_lines": self.malformed_lines,

000014 /// Owner data read from the exact private record published by the manager's
000015 /// module launcher. It is not launch authority until the authenticated
000016 /// `module.hello` succeeds.
000017 pub struct CandidateManagedOwner {
000018     record: Value,
000019     token: String,
000020     boot_id: String,

000024 impl CandidateManagedOwner {
000025     pub fn from_environment() -> Result<Self> {
000026         let state_dir = env::var_os("ELIOT_SWARM_MODULE_STATE")
000027             .map(PathBuf::from)
000028             .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("MANAGED_OWNER_REQUIRED"))?;
000029         let owner_file = env::var_os("ELIOT_SWARM_MODULE_OWNER")
000030             .map(PathBuf::from)
000031             .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("MANAGED_OWNER_REQUIRED"))?;
000032         if !state_dir.is_absolute() || !owner_file.is_absolute() {
000033             return Err(Error::invalid("INVALID_MODULE_OWNER_PATH"));
000034         }
000035         let canonical_state = state_dir
000036             .canonicalize()
000037             .map_err(|_| Error::invalid("INVALID_MODULE_OWNER_PATH"))?;
000038         let canonical_owner = owner_file
000039             .canonicalize()
000040             .map_err(|_| Error::invalid("INVALID_MODULE_OWNER_PATH"))?;
000041         if canonical_owner != canonical_state.join("owner.json") {
000042             return Err(Error::invalid("INVALID_MODULE_OWNER_PATH"));
000043         }
000044 
000045         // Validate the current adapter as a distinct live member of this exact
000046         // published owner group. The helper captures the current process image
000047         // and checks Windows Job/Linux process-group membership; it does not
000048         // treat the owner PID as the adapter or prove family departure.
000049         let proof = verify_current_adapter(&canonical_owner)?;
000050         Self::from_verified_worker(proof)
000051     }
000052 
000053     fn from_verified_worker(proof: VerifiedModuleWorker) -> Result<Self> {
000054         let token = validate_owner_record(&proof.owner_record)?.to_owned();
000055         if !valid_owner_token(&proof.boot_id) {
000056             return Err(Error::invalid("MANAGED_MODULE_OWNER_IDENTITY_INVALID"));
000057         }
000058         Ok(Self {
000059             record: proof.owner_record,
000060             token,
000061             boot_id: proof.boot_id,
000062             adapter_image: proof.process_image_identity,
000063         })
000064     }
000065 
000066     pub fn clone_for_reconnect(&self) -> Self {
000067         Self {
000068             record: self.record.clone(),
000069             token: self.token.clone(),
000070             boot_id: self.boot_id.clone(),
000071             adapter_image: self.adapter_image.clone(),
000072         }
000073     }

000130 fn validate_owner_record(record: &Value) -> Result<&str> {
000131     let encoded_len = serde_json::to_vec(record)
000132         .map_err(|_| Error::invalid("MANAGED_MODULE_OWNER_IDENTITY_INVALID"))?
000133         .len();
000134     let token = record
000135         .get("token")
000136         .and_then(Value::as_str)
000137         .filter(|value| valid_owner_token(value))
000138         .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("MANAGED_OWNER_TOKEN_REQUIRED"))?;
000139     let process = record
000140         .get("process")
000141         .filter(|value| value.is_object())
000142         .ok_or_else(|| Error::invalid("MANAGED_OWNER_PROCESS_REQUIRED"))?;
000143     let valid_scope = matches!(
000144         process.get("scope").and_then(Value::as_str),
000145         Some("windows_job" | "linux_process_group")
000146     );
000147     let has_process_identity = process
000148         .get("pid")
000149         .and_then(Value::as_u64)
000150         .is_some_and(|pid| pid > 0);
000151     if record.get("version").and_then(Value::as_u64) != Some(1)
000152         || encoded_len > 65_536
000153         || process.get("purpose").and_then(Value::as_str) != Some("module")
000154         || !valid_scope
000155         || !has_process_identity
000156     {
000157         return Err(Error::invalid("MANAGED_MODULE_OWNER_IDENTITY_INVALID"));
000158     }
000159     Ok(token)
000160 }
000161 
000162 fn valid_owner_token(value: &str) -> bool {
000163     let bytes = value.as_bytes();
000164     bytes.len() == 36
000165         && [8, 13, 18, 23]
000166             .into_iter()
000167             .all(|index| bytes[index] == b'-')
000168         && bytes
000169             .iter()
000170             .enumerate()
000171             .all(|(index, byte)| [8, 13, 18, 23].contains(&index) || byte.is_ascii_hexdigit())
000172 }
000173 
000174 /// Launches the fixed Antigravity CLI directly after authenticated module
000175 /// ownership has been accepted. Because the module runner starts this adapter
000176 /// in its non-killing group/job and this child is spawned directly without
000177 /// breakaway flags, it inherits that same owner. `Child::wait` below proves
000178 /// only this direct child's exit; family-level departure belongs to the
000179 /// manager-owned `Group::children_empty` check after adapter exit.
000180 pub struct OwnedNativeSpawner {
000181     _owner: VerifiedManagedOwner,
000182 }
000183 
000184 impl OwnedNativeSpawner {
000185     pub fn new(owner: VerifiedManagedOwner) -> Self {
000186         Self { _owner: owner }
000187     }
000188 
000189     pub fn spawn_owned(&self, spec: NativeLaunchSpec) -> Result<OwnedNativeChild> {
000190         if !self._owner.native_launch_allowed {
000191             return Err(Error::new(
000192                 "RECOVERY_REQUIRED",
000193                 "manager requires reconciliation before a native process may start",
000194             ));
000195         }
